BREACH ends when the last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry (`locus_occupants_at_entry`) leaves a locus body that is not the home-ship; a player who was never inside does not trigger it, and while any recorded occupant is still inside the clause is false. **Departure is derivable now** from Ship Body's `actor_section(actor_id)`: `locus_occupants_at_entry` is non-empty and no living seat's `actor_section` is in the recorded `locus_body` (a set of section ids), equivalently `body_of_actor(actor_id) ≠ body_of(locus_section_id)` for every living seat, which makes the rule testable before Web Map exists; Web Map's pod-hop notification (named by Web Map; Save/Load also consumes one, SL event triggers) is the eventual signal and must be repeated by Web Map. If the locus section is cut away or goes dormant mid-event, the locus falls back per AT-14 and the locus body is recomputed; STRESS already armed on a cut-away section lapses on its own hold. **Ordering in BREACH:** the departure test above is evaluated against the recorded `locus_body` before any dormancy fallback in the same run, so a pod departure that empties a non-home locus ends the breach and no fallback occurs (WM:87, Moment 2; E-27).
*Criterion.* AC-AT-46. *Pillar.* P3. *Decided by:* R22.

**AT-23. Co-op seams.** *Rule.* The `source` PlayerId tag is kept; one machine on one host; `repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY`. Whether a downed crewmate's beam or a corpse is a lantern is noted only (EM open question). There is no per-player balance at MVP. *Living* means `loco_state ≠ DEAD`; a DOWNED seat counts as living (AT-31).
*Criterion.* AC-AT-55. *Pillar.* P3. *Decided by:* R23.

**AT-24. Accessibility seams.** *Rule.* Reduce-motion: filament motion becomes a density change. Reduce-flashing: the gutter keeps its spacing (it passes by construction) and the beacon becomes a static mark (LO-26). LO-26 also names a HUD glyph, which contradicts ART:741 and is routed as C27, so the static mark and the audio carry the breach; no glyph is drawn. **No captions are published for tell beats and no caption field exists**: a caption is an Attention indicator in effect (ART:741, :954); whether a generic "[creaking]" category for all hull sounds is acceptable is routed to the accessibility-specialist and the user as an Open Question, and no caption data is designed. With nothing lit there is no visual channel (ART:954 accepts this); the audio channel is sufficient alone, and for a deaf player with any eligible emitter lit (the beam, a fixture, a lantern or a glowstick) the visual channel is sufficient alone.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-47, AC-AT-39. *Pillar.* P2. *Decided by:* R24.

**AT-25. One-way edges and the only writes.** *Rule.* Attention **reads only**: the Emitter State (`emitters()`), the Graph (`power_use`), Ship Body (`actor_section`, `body_of_actor`) and Layout (`body_of`, `sections_of`) lookups, the Controller's `loco_state` (AT-31) and Web Map's ring (provisional). It reads nothing from Lighting, Audio, Sanity, Horror, the Perception Filter, HUD, Instruments, Failure & Trail, Camera or Save/Load. It **writes the world only through two doors**: M17 STRESS and `set_beacon`. Lighting reads only the tier and timing (LO-20 supersedes the index's "Lighting never reads Attention", C12). There are lints in both directions: no Graph, Controller, Suit, Interaction-verb data, camera, Failure & Trail or Item DB code reads an Attention symbol (LSG:281, PCI:1010, SE-25, IA-4, PIPE:151, FT-4, IDB:473). The two soft cycles (Attention ↔ Lighting, Attention ↔ Horror) are therefore resolved one-way.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-48, AC-AT-09. *Pillar.* P1, P4. *Decided by:* R26.

**AT-26. Pre-tell presentation in CALM is quantised and floor-gated.** *Rule.* `level_pm` may drive only **quantised, floor-gated, diegetic density**: ordinary hull-settling creaks and the count of cone-edge filaments, in `CUE_STEPS` (4) steps above `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM` (550, **derived** by the calibration lint below and recorded in Tuning Knobs; the seed value 350 failed that lint and is superseded, C23), with a hysteresis `CUE_HYST_PM` (25) on the way down. **No continuous mapping of `level_pm` to any visible or audible parameter may exist**, because it would be an Attention meter by eye or by ear (ART:741, :64). Pre-tell cues use event ids distinct from tell beats and are excluded from `TellAudit` and from the floor-window count. **Calibration lint (adopted from the audio-director and game-designer, values in Tuning Knobs):** the floor must exceed the steady `level_pm` of a player walking with the beam on by a margin `CUE_MARGIN_PM`, checked per ring; otherwise hull creaks would play whenever the player walks and the tell's onset would blur into pre-tell noise. The computation is in Formulas (F-24): walking with the beam on settles at `B + (1000 + 288) × TAU_ATT_TICKS` mAU, which is 2,618 AU at ring 1 (406 pm) and about 425 pm at ring 3, so the floor is the per-ring maximum plus `CUE_MARGIN_PM` (100), rounded up to 550. **Must-repeat request to Adaptive Audio & Tells (R30):** no music change precedes beat 0; adaptive music must not telegraph the tell earlier than the contract's start tick. That is Audio's rule to repeat, not Attention's.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-49a to AC-AT-49c. *Pillar.* P3. *Decided by:* R27; Audio AA-22 (the pre-tell cue rule).

**AT-27. Persistence and restore.** *Rule.* `post_restore` rebuilds caches (section-id resolution, the sorted section arrays) and **nothing else**: it changes no `phase` and submits no command, because hooks receive `submit_system()` (KRN:593, :609) and a SYSTEM command pending at snapshot time is dropped on reload. The **beacon is reconciled in the first `attention.step` run after a restore**: the run compares `beacon_commanded` (persisted) with the emitter's switch state and submits `set_beacon` **only on a mismatch** (`phase == BREACH` and the beacon off: submit `on`; `phase != BREACH` and it on: submit `off`); no duplicate is submitted when the two already match, and a restore-time mismatch obeys the same `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS` retry as any rejection (AT-18). Cues fire on **transitions only**: loading a save made in any phase triggers no onset, no breach-enter and no release cue. The death stub restores from the newest verified autosave (SL-7) and Attention rolls back with it (WORLD scope); "Attention restores exactly; no meter is stored for display". A restore mid-TELL restores `rng_counter`, `tell_len`, `breach_due_T`, `tell_beat`, `beat_next_off` and `below_hold_runs`, so no length is redrawn and the sequence is hash-identical. Restore coherence: `rng_counter ≤ tell_epoch` (an armed tell increments the epoch but not the counter); a block violating it fails with `ATTENTION_RESTORE_RNG_COUNTER` (AC-AT-70).
*Criterion.* AC-AT-22, AC-AT-23, AC-AT-50, AC-AT-65 (beacon reconciled by the first post-restore run; `post_restore` submits nothing). *Pillar.* P5. *Decided by:* SL-7, SL-8, SL-15; R1.

**AT-28. Determinism and RNG discipline.** *Rule.* Integer-only sim. The only `rng_derive` use is the tell draw. Beat jitter and every presentation draw are pure integer hashes of `(tell_epoch, k, HASH_SEED)`; the hash is an ADR candidate shared with Lighting (LO-19). All sums are 64-bit and bounded; Attention uses sim `T` only, never wall time. A paused game stops `T` and so stops Attention.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-22, AC-AT-12. *Pillar.* kernel G3, G4.

**AT-29. Event-defer coupling to Save/Load.** *Rule.* One event never forces a RISKY autosave: `TELL_MAX_TICKS + BREACH_MAX_TICKS + SUBSIDE_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS < AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (all cited or from knobs; registry formula `event_defer_invariant`), and the CALM gap between chained events is at least one retry (`REFRACT_TICKS ≥ AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`, `TELL_REARM_TICKS ≥ AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`). Re-check across every `gate_tell_mode` (FAIR and STRICT) whenever any term changes. Placeholders: 1800 + 3600 + 1200 + 300 = 6900, below the cited cap; the lint therefore binds `BREACH_MAX_TICKS` from above.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-51, AC-AT-52. *Pillar.* P5. *Decided by:* R12; SL:190-193.

**AT-30. MVP scope.** *Rule.* MVP = one threshold, one tier of lamp tell, one locus rule, one breach content (STRESS plus beacon), one noise-only push API. **Vertical Slice addenda, not absorbed:** multiple tiers, per-ring thresholds, ring baselines beyond ring 1 and the per-ring stimulus gain (a pillar-flagged requirement, AT-6), the suppressant verb, per-player attribution, rate-coupled tell length, co-op scaling, Knowledge Codex recognition of a decoded tell, and a scripted onboarding tell. Phantom tells and false calm are not on this list: they are rejected by pillar (AT-17), permanently.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-61 (review). *Pillar.* all.

**AT-31. Seat state read (added by the author stage; flagged).** *Rule.* "Living player" (AT-6, AT-23) and the pod hold (AT-6, AT-22) need the Controller's `loco_state` per bound seat: a seat is *living* if `loco_state ≠ DEAD` (DOWNED counts as living) and *in pod* if `loco_state == IN_POD`. This is an ordered read of the Controller's `controller.state` (no `after` needed: `controller.step` precedes the hook transitively) and is **added to the read list of AT-25**, which the rulings' one-way list omitted. It creates no cycle (nothing in the Controller reads Attention, PCI:1010). The Controller owner is asked to repeat that `loco_state` is a published SIM read.
*Criterion.* AC-AT-55. *Pillar.* P3.

**AT-32. Diagnostics are inert.** *Rule.* A DIAGNOSTIC subscriber records, per run and per session, the counts of TELL entries, aborts and breaches, ticks in each phase, the per-channel contribution (noise, light, power) of `I_run` over the ticks preceding each TELL entry, abort latency, and locus occupancy at breach. These values are never read by sim code, never published in `attention.view`, never shown to the player and are excluded from `state_hash`. They exist so a designer can answer "what crossed the line" without a meter in the game. (ADR candidate: diagnostics pipeline and export format.)
*Criterion.* AC-AT-56 (hash identical with diagnostics on and off). *Pillar.* P3.

### States and Transitions

*The machine has four states. Every transition is evaluated inside `attention.step`, after the run's integration (AT-5) and in the order below; `post_restore` changes no `phase` and submits no command (the beacon is reconciled in the first `attention.step` run after a restore, AT-27). "Who may cause it" lists the only causes: no PLAYER-origin command, no other system's mutator and no timer outside the block can move the machine, apart from the SYSTEM-origin `attention_arm_tell` seam.*

#### State table

| State | Entry condition (checked in the run, after integrating) | Entry actions | While in state | Exit condition and next | Who may cause it |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **CALM** (initial; `default_state`: `phase` CALM, `L = B`, all timers 0, `tell_outcome` NONE, counters 0) | boot; TELL abort; SUBSIDING end | after an abort: `tell_outcome = ABORTED`, `rearm_until_T = T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`. After SUBSIDING: `tell_outcome = BREACHED`, `refract_until_T = T + REFRACT_TICKS` | integrate (AT-5); publish `level_pm`; pre-tell cues quantised (AT-26); `tell_active` 0 | F-9 true (AT-7: `L ≥ theta`, past both timers) → TELL | the level (stimuli) crossing theta; or `attention_arm_tell` (SYSTEM) |
| **TELL** | entry guard of AT-7, or `attention_arm_tell` | `tell_epoch += 1`; `tell_start_T = T`; `tell_len` drawn (AT-8); `breach_due_T = T + tell_len`; locus and `locus_body` fixed (AT-14); `tell_beat = 0` **published in this run**; `tier = 1`; `below_hold_runs = 0` | integrate; beats on the elapsed-keyed schedule (AT-9); tier by elapsed; `tell_remaining = max(0, breach_due_T − T)`; abort counter (AT-10) | abort rule (AT-10) → CALM; at the first run with `T ≥ breach_due_T`: `L ≥ theta_HOLD` → BREACH, else → CALM (abort) | the level and the clock only. A pod hop does not interrupt it |
| **BREACH** | the TELL due run with `L ≥ theta_HOLD` | AT-11: STRESS deposits for the locus body, `set_beacon(on)` at T+1; `breach_enter_T = T`; `clear_runs = 0`; record `locus_occupants_at_entry` = the set of living seats whose `actor_section` is in `locus_body` at the BREACH-entry run | integrate with `W_L[FAULT_GLOW] = W_L[BEACON] = 0`; STRESS bursts (AT-12); `exogenous_permitted` true for the locus body | the first of `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`; `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` then quiet for `CLEAR_HOLD_TICKS`; pod-hop departure (the last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry leaving a non-home locus body); empty non-home locus body at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` → SUBSIDING | the level, the clock, and (for departure) the last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry leaving the recorded `locus_body` |
| **SUBSIDING** | BREACH end | `set_beacon(off)`; stop deposits; `L ← min(L, B + POST_PM × (theta − B) / 1000)`; `subside_end_T = T + SUBSIDE_TICKS` | integrate normally; `exogenous_permitted` stays true for the locus body | `T ≥ subside_end_T` → CALM | the clock only |

**Illegal or ignored transitions.** CALM → BREACH (none); TELL → SUBSIDING (none); BREACH → TELL (none); `attention_arm_tell` outside CALM is rejected `NOT_CALM`. A second crossing of theta in the same run (two LOUD pushes in co-op) produces one TELL, one `tell_epoch` increment and one RNG draw.

#### Transition guards

| Transition | Guard | Actions |
|---|---|---|
| CALM → TELL | `phase == CALM AND L ≥ theta AND T ≥ refract_until_T AND T ≥ rearm_until_T` (inclusive) | AT-7, AT-8, AT-9 entry; no catch-up elapsed time is credited if the level sat above theta during a timer |
| TELL → CALM (abort, early) | `below_hold_runs × 6 ≥ ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` | `tell_outcome = ABORTED`; no STRESS; no beacon; `L` unchanged (or `ABORT_RELIEF_PM`, default no relief); `rearm_until_T = T + TELL_REARM_TICKS` |
| TELL → CALM (abort, at due) | `T ≥ breach_due_T AND L < theta_HOLD` | same as above; wins over a simultaneous BREACH decision |
| TELL → BREACH | `T ≥ breach_due_T AND L ≥ theta_HOLD` | AT-11 |
| BREACH → SUBSIDING | AT-12 (i) to (iv) | AT-13 entry |
| SUBSIDING → CALM | `T ≥ subside_end_T` | `tell_outcome = BREACHED`; `refract_until_T = T + REFRACT_TICKS` |

#### Published fields by state

| Field | CALM | TELL | BREACH | SUBSIDING |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tell_active` | 0 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| `breach_phase` (Save/Load only) | CALM | **CALM** | BREACH | SUBSIDING |
| `tell_remaining` | 0 | `tell_len` down to 0 (non-increasing within an epoch; the field's range is 0 to `TELL_MAX_TICKS`) | 0 | 0 |
| `tell_elapsed` | 0 | 0 up | 0 | 0 |
| `tier` | 0 | 1 to 3 | 0 | 0 |
| `tell_beat` | frozen | counts up from 0 | frozen | frozen |
| `exogenous_permitted(locus body)` | false | false | **true** | **true** |
| beacon (commanded) | off | off | **on** (home-ship's single beacon) | off |
| STRESS deposits | none | none | at entry and in bursts | none (existing arming lapses on the Graph's hold) |
| `W_L[FAULT_GLOW]` | 1000 | 1000 | **0** | **0** |

#### Persisted timers and counters (all in `attention.state`; see AT-1)

| Item | Set at | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `tell_start_T`, `tell_len`, `breach_due_T` | TELL entry | the seeded, fixed window; `tell_len ≥ TELL_MIN_TICKS` on every path |
| `rng_counter` | each TELL draw | the only `rng_derive` use; restores with the block (no reroll) |
| `below_hold_runs` | each TELL run | consecutive runs under theta_HOLD |
| `rearm_until_T` | abort | no new TELL before this tick |
| `breach_enter_T`, `clear_runs`, `stress_next_burst_run`, `stress_pending_cursor` | BREACH | duration, quiet counter, STRESS schedule |
| `locus_occupants_at_entry` | BREACH entry | the set of living seats whose `actor_section` was in `locus_body` at the BREACH-entry run; the reference set for the pod-hop clause (AT-12 iii, F-17) and the X10 clause (AT-12 iv); persisted (AT-1) so a restore mid-BREACH keeps both clauses |
| `subside_end_T`, `refract_until_T` | SUBSIDING, SUBSIDING end | valley timers |
| `tell_beat`, `beat_next_off` | each beat | beat counter and the nominal offset of the next beat |
| `locus_section_id`, `locus_body`, `held_ring`, `beacon_commanded`, `beacon_retry_left` | TELL entry, pod hop, BREACH entry/exit, a rejected `set_beacon` | scoping and the beacon request state; `beacon_retry_left` counts down from `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS` and is what the first post-restore run reconciles against (AT-27) |

### Interactions with Other Systems

*Data flow is from Attention's perspective ("in" = to Attention, "out" = from Attention). Where a counterpart GDD exists its own Interactions or Dependencies row for Attention is **quoted** (section id, then the line on disk on 2026-10-26) so the two documents can be checked against each other (bidirectional consistency). Rows about a system whose GDD is absent are **provisional** and end with what that GDD **must repeat**. This GDD never edits another document: every wording drift is routed as a "refresh owed" in Cross-References (C1 to C25).*

#### Upstream — designed

| System | Data in | Data out | Interface owner and exact names | Counterpart's text (quoted) | Must repeat / refresh owed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Command & Simulation Layer** *(designed)* | the tick base; `rng_derive(world_seed, stream, counter)`; `ticks_for_seconds`; `WriteScope`; SYSTEM-origin commands (processed T+1); ordered `read_view` | block `attention.state`; hook `attention.step`; mutator `push_stimulus`; command `attention_arm_tell` | the kernel owns CR-3, CR-8, CR-9, CR-12; Attention registers | Interactions row 13 (KRN:205): "Attention block (`SIM`, `persist`), stimulus intake, breach state machine hook at divisor 6 \| Ordered reads of Emitter exposure, Graph and Suit; tick base for the tell window \| Attention \| Missing from the index's kernel edges (index correction candidate)". Dependencies (KRN:692): "Attention System \| Hard \| Registry, step hook (breach state machine) \| Attention block, stimulus intake \| **No — add edge**" | **Refreshes owed:** row 13 "reads … Suit" (Attention reads none of Suit at MVP, SE-31); row 4 (KRN:196) "pushes noise/power stimuli to Attention" (Suit pushes none); the D.10 example stream `"horror.tell"` (Attention uses `"attention.tell"`; Horror keeps its own, C8); the stagger example "Graph 0, Attention 2, Suit 4" (Attention is at offset 0, C3); the fixture note (KRN:948) to be re-pointed at the real block |
| **Life-Support Graph** *(designed)* | `power_use(section_id) = {draw_w, delta_w_last5, lamps_lit_w, noisy_w}` read after `graph.propagate.power` | M17 `deposit_trace(STRESS, section, BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT)`; nothing else | LS-8, LS-13, LS-19, LS-25, M17; `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS`; `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`; `W_MIN_WARNING_S`; the Graph owns failure physics, the field semantics and the STRESS effect, Attention owns *when* STRESS is armed | Interactions row (LSG:267): "Attention System \| M17 STRESS at the breach-rhythm transition (via Horror or directly) \| `power_use(section_id)` as a stimulus \| Graph publishes; Attention reads `power_use` (routed) \| Attention `after: graph.propagate.power` \| provisional". LS-13 exported requirement: "every non-player exogenous source … must therefore be preceded by that system's own Pillar 2 tell of 10–30 s … The Graph neither emits nor checks that tell" | **Routed asks to the Graph owner:** (1) define the four `power_use` fields and state whether `delta_w_last5` excludes `lamps_lit_w` (C11); (2) refresh the M17-versus-D.9 STRESS-magnitude wording (C15); (3) once this GDD is accepted, state the STRESS caller as "Attention directly at BREACH entry; Horror per its GDD" (the current "via Horror or directly" is accurate and is not a conflict). No Graph formula takes Attention as input (LSG:281). The Graph's endogenous gate is not how this contract is enforced (AT-15) |
| **Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query** *(designed in this run)* | `emitters()` (id, kind, `section_id`, `output_pm`, `reach_px`); EM D.12 per-emitter term (cited); staleness rule EM-25 | `set_beacon {emitter_id, on}` as a SYSTEM command at T+1; confirmation of `max_age_ticks` 120; concurrence (not a decision) that BEACON `gates_actions` is false | `set_beacon`, `emitter_kind`, `beacon_aim`, `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`, EM-21, EM-25, EM-27; the Emitter State owns the records, `lit` and `lux_pm` and the beacon's aim; Attention owns units, weights, thresholds and the breach machine | Interactions row (EM:329): "`set_beacon` (`SYSTEM`) from the breach machine \| the per-emitter list; `section_emission(section_id)`; subject `lux(ref)` for each player (the owner's own beam counts once, at its apex value, EM-21) \| Attention owns units, weights, thresholds and the breach machine; Emitter State owns the light data \| Attention `after: [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]` (requested) \| provisional … no double count with `lamps_lit_w`". Dependencies (EM:751): "Hard \| the per-emitter list, `section_emission`, `lux(ref)`, `set_beacon`" | **Confirmed here:** `after: [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]` and `max_age_ticks` 120. **Concurred, not decided:** BEACON `gates_actions` false (the registry row says "provisional, user decision pending" and EM:200 routes the decision to the technical-director and game-designer, C21). Attention uses `emitters()` only (no `section_emission` call, no `lux`, no `probe`): the registry `MAX_PROBES_PER_TICK` note already says Attention makes no probe() call and does not read it (refreshed 2026-10-27; no refresh owed). The "provisional (Attention undesigned)" markers on EM's rows are discharged by this text |

Interaction's open question on weld and cut sparks (IA OQ 18): sparks stay **non-emissive at MVP**, so they are not a light stimulus; reversible, a decision for the Emitter State and this GDD in the Vertical Slice |

The Suit never reads Attention (AC-SE-26). **Refresh owed:** index edge Attention → Suit downgraded to soft, none at MVP (C14); the "ordered read of `view(player_id)`" in Suit's rows becomes "none at MVP" |

#### Designed neighbours that name Attention (not in the index dependency list)

| System | Data in | Data out | Interface owner and exact names | Counterpart's text (quoted) | Must repeat / refresh owed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
