**Index corrections routed (this GDD never edits the index):** Attention ← Interaction (soft; Interaction is a push source); Attention ← Controller (soft; already noted in the index's design note); Attention ← Ship Body / Layout (soft); Attention ← Web Map & Transit (soft); Attention → Suit downgraded to soft, none at MVP; Lighting ← Attention reworded from "never reads" to "reads the published tier and timing only" (C12); the rationale bullet's "pushed in by Graph, Suit, … Assembly" (C6) and "flicker" (C10). The Dependency Map's "[bottleneck: 5 dependents]" is Horror Entities, Sanity, Adaptive Audio, Depth Rings and the Knowledge Codex; Lighting and Save/Load read Attention but are listed as dependencies elsewhere.

**Circularity check.** Attention reads the Graph's power phase (ordered `after: graph.propagate.power`) and calls its Class B mutator; the Graph reads nothing from Attention (LSG:281). Attention reads the Emitter State's list (ordered `after: emitter.place`) and submits a SYSTEM command at T+1; the Emitter State's only read of Attention is none. Lighting reads Attention's published tier and Attention reads nothing Lighting produces (LO:948), which resolves the index's soft cycle one-way. Horror's hook is `after` Attention and Attention reads nothing from Horror (Horror pushes no noise, HE-36; had it any, Class B calls inside a `WriteScope` would be inputs, not reads). Sanity reads Attention and Attention reads nothing from Sanity. The Controller pushes noise and Attention reads only `loco_state`; nothing in the Controller reads Attention. No cycle exists in the hook DAG: `suit.vitals → controller.step → body.integrate → emitter.place → attention.step`, and `graph.propagate.power → attention.step`, with Horror, Audio's polling subscriber and the Presenter downstream.

**Engine reference gaps (Godot 4.7.2, pinned; probed 2026-10-25).** `docs/engine-reference/godot/` holds `VERSION.md`, `breaking-changes.md`, `current-best-practices.md`, `deprecated-apis.md` and `modules/` (animation, audio, input, navigation, networking, physics, rendering, ui). **There is no `scripting.md` or `core.md`:** GDScript 64-bit integer arithmetic and the integer-division behaviour for negative operands, `RefCounted` block performance, `Dictionary` iteration order and signal semantics are NOT SOURCEABLE from the reference, so every cost number in this GDD is a model parameter for a spike and every determinism claim that rests on iteration order says "written, not assumed" (section-keyed arrays stored sorted by `section_id`). The domain is a pure-integer headless SIM block, so the reference is silent on the system; `audio.md` (tell sounds) and `rendering.md` (filament drawing) were consulted. Sourced 4.7 hazards that bite: a typed-return override of `read_view()` needs an explicit `return` on every path (HIGH); a packed-array element write no longer calls the property setter, so dirty keys are tracked explicitly in the mutators (HIGH); `CanvasItem` line drawing has no antialiasing feather, so 1-art-px filaments must be verified at every zoom rung (HIGH); `AudioStreamPlayer2D.area_mask` now defaults to 0, so any area-based bus override silently does nothing until set (MEDIUM, installed editor is 4.7.2 so the risk is live); `duplicate()` is not deep (MEDIUM); the renderer default and glow ordering affect any glow-dependent iridescence (MEDIUM, the lighting spike decides). No ADR exists yet (`docs/architecture/` holds only `tr-registry.yaml`); the ADR candidates raised here are: a pure integer hash shared with Lighting for presentation randomness; presentation-subscriber polling by counter comparison; the block base class and `read_view()` return discipline under 4.7; filament rendering at 1 art px; audio voice reservation and the SubViewport listener policy (Audio's).

## Tuning Knobs

*Every number in this section is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` unless a row says "derived", "fixed" or "cited". Data lives in `assets/data/attention/{intake, integrator, weights, baseline, tell, breach, presentation}.json` (the audio-side knobs live in Adaptive Audio's `assets/data/audio/tell_audio.json` and are pointers here). **★ marks a registry candidate**: a cross-system fact another GDD reads, cites or must keep consistent (the registry obligations are listed in Cross-References). "Safe range" is the range the lints of Formulas allow at the placeholder rows; where a lint, not taste, sets the edge, the edge is computed with the integer model of F-15 at the **ring-3 test row** (the tightest row: `B` 550 AU with the two wake glows, `x_dark` 1,209 AU, 98 runs against a limit of 99). Each row names its owner in parentheses: SD systems-designer, GD game-designer, AD audio-director, TD technical-director, ART art-director.*

#### Intake and the unit table

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `U_TOOL` ★ (SD; `intake`) | 4 | AU per accrued tool tick | 2 to 6 (IR-1) | tool work is as loud as running ×1.5; every cut is a tell | tool verbs feel free; no cost to working | IR-1, IR-2, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`; the 180-tick patch = 720 AU |
| `U_LOUD` ★ (SD; `intake`) | 180 (= 30 × 6) | AU per one-shot | `30 × U_TOOL` to 400; **GD alternative 600** (CQ-6) | one bang is a threshold event, nothing else matters | a bang is 2.7 percent of the span and inert | IR-2, the chain-crossing trace (F-25), `ATT_THRESHOLD` |
| `QUIET` unit | 0 | AU | fixed at 0 | n/a | n/a | the class exists so a silent verb is declared |
| `NOISE_PER_TICK` walk / run / carry | cited (Controller; 1 / 4 / 1) | AU per tick | **not Attention's**: see the registry | n/a | n/a | the unit definition (1 AU = one walking tick) |

#### Integrator, threshold and hold

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `TAU_ATT_TICKS` ★ (SD; `integrator`) | 1800 | ticks | 1200 to 1816 at the ring-3 test row with the two wake glows (1817 fails F-15; 3600 is the arithmetic ceiling, F-5) | the whole sortie matters; the abort needs longer tells; ring 3 fails abortability | everything is forgotten in seconds; job-splitting launders (IR-3 binds at 300) | IR-3, F-15, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`, `TELL_DRAW_LO` |
| `ATT_THRESHOLD` ★ (SD/GD; `integrator`) | 6000 | AU | 5816 to 12000 (the ring-3 lint with the two wake glows fails at 5815 and below); **GD alternative 7200** | breaches too rare, the web is toothless | breach in ordinary play; walking with the beam sits near the line | `B_table`, `HOLD_PM`, `U[RUN] × tau` (running alone settles at B + 7,200 AU, so 7200 sits just under that asymptote), the cue ladder |
| `HOLD_PM` ★ (SD; `integrator`) | 800 | permille | 798 to 900 at the ring-3 row with the two wake glows (797 fails) | the abort barely clears the line; tells re-enter fast | the abort needs a long deep quiet; F-15 fails | `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS`, F-15, the breach clear rule. **Open question:** a baseline-relative hold line (GD-3) |
| `L_CEIL_PM` ★ (SD; `integrator`) | 1020 | permille | 1005 to 1022 at the ring-3 row with the two wake glows (1023 fails) | abortability fails (entry too high) | the level plateaus early; `level_pm` saturates before `theta` | F-15, F-5. ADDITION X1, adopted by checkpoint ruling CQ-2 and approved at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate |
| `ATT_MARGIN` ★ (SD; `integrator`) | 50 | AU | 0 to 62 at the ring-3 row with the two wake glows (63 fails) | the baseline cap shrinks; ring 3 fails | the proof has no slack | F-15, F-16 |
| `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` ★ (SD; `tell`) | 120 | ticks (a multiple of 6) | 60 to 300 | an early abort is slow; the at-due path dominates | a one-run dip aborts a tell | F-14, `HOLD_PM`; Audio's `RELEASE_TICKS` (90) must stay below it |
| `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` (GD; `tell`) | 1000 (no relief) | permille of the span | 600 to 1000 | no effect | aborts become rewarding; tell farming | `TELL_REARM_TICKS`; the lever for chained-abort fatigue only |

#### Light and power weights

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `W_L[BEAM]` ★ (SD; `weights`) | 1000 | micro-AU per unit per tick | 1000 to 3000 (GD felt alternative about 2000) | lamp-on dominates; darkness becomes the only play | the lamp is free; going dark is not a trade | IA-6 lit-only rule; F-3 derivation |
| `W_L[FIXTURE]`, `W_L[NODE_LANTERN]` ★ (SD; `weights`) | 1000 each | micro-AU per unit per tick | 300 to 1200 (GD alternative about 700) | the home ship's rest floor climbs; growth hits the ceiling early (GD-15) | "the lights you left on" has no cost | F-16, the Vertical Slice growth tripwire |
| `W_L[DEPLOYABLE]` ★ (SD; `weights`) | 1000 | micro-AU per unit per tick | 500 to 4500 (GD felt alternative about 3000, CQ-6) | glowsticks unusable | below 500: glowsticks are nearly free light | `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`, the wane (EM D.9) |

| `W_N` (`noisy_w`) | 0 | micro-AU per W per tick | 0 (reserved) | n/a | n/a | C11: `noisy_w` has no definition anywhere |

*Interaction notes.* (1) The ring-3 test row is deliberately near the cliff of F-15: tau 1,817, `HOLD_PM` 797, `L_CEIL_PM` 1,023, `ATT_MARGIN` 63, `W_P` 117 and a ring-3 baseline of 595 AU each fail it singly (with the two wake glows), so several of these cannot move together; the lint, not this table, is the authority. (2) `ATT_THRESHOLD` and `U_LOUD` trade off: at 6000 and 180 the AC-AT-57f chain trace crosses in about 50 seconds from the home-idle level (about 60 seconds from `B`; integer F-5 run, 3,000 ticks); the game-designer's 7200 and 600 cross at the same fifth job end on that fixture (about 50 seconds) with a larger span; the harness fixes the pair. (3) The occupancy lever order if playtests show a siege: `REFRACT_TICKS`, then `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, never `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT`. (4) The candidates with a ★ are registered in Cross-References; none of the Graph's, the Controller's or the Emitter's constants is.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*Lane.* Attention defines **no sound and no sprite**. It publishes facts (AT-16) and owns one presenter (AT-20); the pixels of the beacon belong to Emitter State and the Derelict Authoring kit, the lamp gutter to 2D Lighting, the failure trails to Failure & Trail, and every sound, mix and cue name to Adaptive Audio & Tells (Designed, `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`: every audio line below is a **requirement on that GDD**, which repeats it in its own AA rules). This section is the art-director's and audio-director's proposals folded into one set. **Requirement ids.** `AV-n` (visual) and `AUD-n` (audio) are *requirements under AT-9, AT-20, AT-24 and AT-26*, not Core Rules; they keep the specialists' ids so a citation into their spec files stays legible. Every presenter number below is an **art value `[PLACEHOLDER]`**, lives in `assets/data/attention/presentation.json`, is not a simulation constant, is not a registry entry and never feeds `state_hash` (changing one never invalidates a save). Art-bible citations are by section id (`ART 2.7`) with the lines used elsewhere in this GDD (`ART:741`, `:954`).

*Orchestrator and gate rulings in this section.* (1) *(gate ruling, CD-GDD-ALIGN, supersedes the earlier orchestrator ruling "inside a lit cone": strands resolve at the edge of the lit region of a player-controllable real emitter, kinds `BEAM`, `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN` and `DEPLOYABLE`; `BEACON`, the cryo EMERGENCY lamp `fx_cryo_lamp` and `FAULT_GLOW` are excluded; strands are never drawn in unlit space. Cone-only is a producer scope fallback only. Every "lit region" below is this reading; AT-20 holds the definition. Consequence: with the chest lamp off but fixtures lit the visual channel remains; with nothing lit there is none, which `ART:954` accepts.)* (2) *(orchestrator ruling: strand counts are the Tuning Knobs `FILAMENT_STRANDS[tier]` 2 / 4 / 7 per lit region, which sit inside the art-director's bands 1 to 2 / 3 to 5 / 6 to 9; `STRAND_VIEW_CAP` 9 is the hard ceiling.*) (3) *(orchestrator ruling: hull-stress hairlines are cut from the Tell permanently, not only from MVP, as the art-director recommends; a Vertical Slice revisit is allowed only if the hairline can be made unconfusable with both the Integrity trail and a hidden-entity footprint, ART 5.5 (v).)*

### Events: what the player sees and hears

*Reading key.* "Source" is the published fact the presenter or Audio reads (`attention.view`, AT-16). **Class T** is the truth channel (AT-17): derived from SIM fields, never from `PERCEPTION_OUT`, never added, removed, delayed or altered by the Perception Filter or Sanity. "Owner" names who authors the pixels or the sound; Attention owns only the rows marked *Presenter*. Priority: **P0** = required for the MVP contract (a tell the player could have perceived), **P1** = required for MVP feel, **P2** = polish or cuttable.

Step *n* raises the stub count in whole sites. ### Visual requirements (AV-n)

**AV-1. Nothing the player controls changes.** In every phase the beam radius, temperature, direction, colour, the global ambient level, the camera (position, zoom, shake), the controls and the HUD are identical (ART 2.7: "the beam is a truth anchor, and attention is not sanity"; PIPE:151, PCI:135). The presenter writes only `CanvasItem`, `Material` and `Light2D`-adjacent properties and never calls into gameplay (ART 8.11 control-manifest rule). Lint: AC-AT-53, AC-AT-48.

**AV-2. Motion vocabulary: stepped, not tweened.** All strand motion is stepped at the horror-rig playback rate (12 fps, every 5th tick; ART 5.4, 8.4, 8.10); web pieces may sit off-grid. No sub-pixel blur; dust is nearest-filtered pixels, never soft alpha (ART 8.3); the only permitted rotation of a web piece is the 15 degree quantisation of ART 5.4.

**AV-3. Rate and amplitude ceilings.** Resolve events at most 2 per second per lit region (derived from `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS`); twitch at most once per two beats per strand; sway at most 1 Hz; one dust impulse per beat. No luminance change over a region larger than one strand's footprint repeats above 3 Hz anywhere in the world view (ART 7.7 HR-13, adopted world-side as a design ceiling; the accessibility-specialist confirms). The band-slide is a travelling highlight on a strand, not a flash. No Attention visual shakes, scales or shifts the frame.

**AV-4. The presenter is a pure function of published state.** Its output depends only on `phase`, `tell_epoch`, `tell_beat`, `tier`, `phase_enter_T`, `breach_enter_T`, the quantised cue step, the lit-region geometry and a pure integer hash of `(tell_epoch, site id, HASH_SEED)`. It never reads `tell_remaining`, never infers the breach moment, never reads Audio and never draws from `rng_derive` (AT-20, AT-28, AT-16 lints). Presentation need not be replay-identical across a restore (it is not sim state), but beat count, tier and phase parity must hold.

**AV-5. A beat has one visual signature: the band-slide.** A beat renders as a **resolve** (stub or hidden site to full strand in `STRAND_RESOLVE_FRAMES`, 4 stepped frames) during which a single iridescent specular **band slides from root to tip** once: the art-bible 9.4 behaviour (the band moves with the angle) compressed into one gesture. It is the *only* animation that means "beat". Stubs never slide; footprints, hallucinated web and the ring's resident web never use it (AV-18).

**AV-6. Pre-tell cues are quantised and strictly weaker than the tell (AT-26).** The stub count is a function of the *quantised step* (0 to `CUE_STEPS`) only. The maximum stub density (`STUB_MAX_PM`) is **below** the strand density at tell beat 0 (`DENSITY_BASE_PM`), stubs are a **nested subset** of the strands that appear at onset (so the onset reads as the same watchers leaning in), and stubs are static. No continuous mapping of `level_pm` exists in any visible parameter: not density, size, brightness, band intensity or dust amplitude.

**AV-7. Every beat is visible in every lit region (the visual half of the two-channel criterion, AT-9).** Per site: `resolved(site) = site_hash(site, tell_epoch) mod 1000 < density_pm(tell_beat)`, with `density_pm(b) = min(DENSITY_MAX_PM, DENSITY_BASE_PM + DENSITY_STEP_PM × b)`. Density is a function of `tell_beat` alone, which is itself a function of `tell_elapsed` alone; it never depends on `tell_len`, `tell_remaining` or any progress value (no leak of the end). **Guaranteed resolve:** at each beat, in every lit region that has an unresolved eligible site, if none newly crossed the threshold inside the cone's rim band, the nearest unresolved site to the rim is force-resolved. A lit region with no eligible site on screen is the only legal exception, and the harness logs it (AC-AT-107). A site is eligible only if it lies in the rim band of the lit region of an emitter of kind `BEAM`, `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN` or `DEPLOYABLE`; the rim band of a non-cone region is `RIM_BAND_PM` of that emitter's reach (AV-14).

**AV-8. Tier is a motion class, not a count (AT-9).** Per-cone strand counts are art intents (`FILAMENT_STRANDS[tier]` 2 / 4 / 7, never more than `STRAND_VIEW_CAP`, because strands are a supporting shape, ART 3.5, and must never bury a failing pipe or a trail). Tier 1 = **drift** (resolve and hold; a dust impulse of one base unit); tier 2 = **twitch** (a 1 art-px tip offset, stepped, irregular by hash; dust impulse doubled); tier 3 = **sway** (a stepped lean between `held` and `lean`; dust impulse tripled). "Drift, then movement" (ART 2.7) is literal. Boundaries are the published `tier`; the presenter holds no timer of its own.

**AV-9. Release is slow and quiet.** `STRAND_RELEASE_TICKS` equals Audio's `RELEASE_TICKS` (90, the value is Audio's; this GDD requires only the equality). The release is the reverse of the resolve, stepped, with no band-slide.

**AV-10. The held rim.** At BREACH entry the strands snap taut in `TAUT_SNAP_FRAMES` (2) stepped frames, then are **absolutely still** until SUBSIDING. Reason (ART 2.8 readability rule): the breach's motion budget belongs to trails, vents and the sweep, and a sudden stillness at the rim is a distinct, learnable, non-red phase cue that works on a derelict where the single home-ship beacon is not in view. Reduce-motion leaves it unchanged (it is already still).

**AV-11. No "all clear" and no "success" visual.** Not at abort, not at SUBSIDING, not at CALM. The rim relaxes; nothing celebrates. The consequence channel (trails, frost, scorch, a running pipe) is the only thing that says the ship is or is not all right, and STRESS may outlast SUBSIDING (LSG:986, AT-12). The presenter draws nothing when `phase == CALM` and the Graph still holds STRESS; the Graph's trails carry it.

**AV-12. Restore, pause, hitch.** On the first rendered frame after a restore the presenter draws the phase-correct end state with all transition animation suppressed, reconstructs resolved sites from `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` and the hash (never from stored presentation state), and reconciles the beacon lens from the emitter's switch state. During a pause or a sim stall nothing animates. After a hitch it never replays missed beats: it renders the current `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` once.

**AV-13. Zoom, camera, resolution.** Strands, dust and the beacon are world-space, integer-scaled sprites that scale with the zoom ladder like any web piece; no zoom compensation, no screen-space sizing. A resolved strand survives the 50 percent scale test of ART 5.6 and 8.2 (trunk at least 2 art px tapering to 1 px at the tip), so it still reads at the zoom-out rung. The camera does nothing (PIPE:151).

**AV-14. "Lit" (the lit region of a player-controllable real emitter; gate ruling, widened from cone-only).** A strand is drawn only inside the lit region of an emitter of kind `BEAM` (the cone), `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN` or `DEPLOYABLE` (a pool), with a rim band of `RIM_BAND_PM` of the emitter's reach. `BEACON`, the cryo EMERGENCY lamp `fx_cryo_lamp` and `FAULT_GLOW` never carry a strand. Unlit space draws no strand at all: lit-pass sprites, **not** a mask (Principle 1). The HR-15 low-vision ambient-floor option raises hull only and never reveals strands (ART 7.7 HR-15). Cone-only is a producer scope fallback for MVP, recorded as scope and not as creative direction.

**AV-15. Strands are not emitters and not Attention input.** They are lit sprites with a specular band; they **cast no light, raise no Attention and are never in `emitters()`** (the same rule ART 5.5 gives the sanity fringe). A band that contributed light would feed the level that drives it, the same loop that sets `W_L[BEACON]` to 0. Lint: no presenter output is registered as an emitter and no Attention input reads a presenter value (AC-AT-110).

**AV-16. Depth separation: the ring baseline is static, the tell is an event.** At the middle and inner rings the resident web already "holds the light" and "glitters at its edge" (ART 4.3). The tell must therefore be identifiable *without* reading brightness: the resident web never changes pose except for the slow band-slide the player's own beam angle causes (ART 9.4); only tell strands (`web_strand_*`) change pose on a beat (AC-AT-108).

**AV-17. Every visual in the event table is class T.** The MVP has no phantom strand, no false calm and no phantom beacon; phantom tells and false calm are rejected by pillar, permanently, at every tier (AT-17). The Perception Filter's anchor list includes the tell cues (AC-AT-41).

**AV-18. Exclusive signature.** The `web_strand_*` sheets, the band-slide-on-beat animation and the stub reveal are **exclusive to the Attention Presenter**. Sanity, Horror, the Perception Filter and the ring's resident web must not reuse them: the visual twin of the audio rule that Sanity's audio uses a family disjoint from the tell's. Lint by asset-tag family (AC-AT-113).

### Truth channel and corruptible channels

| Visual or audio | Class | Why and consequence |
|---|---|---|
| Stubs, resolved strands, band-slide, twitch, sway, taut hold, release, dust impulse | **T** | Drawn from `attention.view` only; global and beat-locked |
| Lamp gutter | **T** | Lighting executes it from the published tier |
| Beacon lens and sweep | **T** | Emitter State executes `set_beacon`; a lamp is a physical object |
| Failure trails driven by STRESS | **T** (consequence channel) | Failure & Trail's; this system neither shows nor hides Attention there (FT-4) |
| The bed, beats, release, alarm | **T** | Audio's; the two senses share one counter (`tell_beat`) |
| Sanity violet fringe at the cone rim, shadows with no occluder, false reach | **Corruptible** (per viewer) | Sanity's (ART 4.4 stage 2). Never an Attention visual |
| Phantom entities, phantom web content | **Corruptible** | Sanity and Horror; they may not use Attention's assets, animation or audio family |

**The cone-rim contention (resolved).** Sanity stage 2 puts a violet fringe at the cone rim, the same place the strands resolve (ART 2.7). Both are "something at the edge of my light". They are made unconfusable on **hue, shape, motion, scope and falsifiability**, each sufficient alone: *hue* (strands teal to green to bronze to gold on a Void Steel mass, low saturation, versus violet `#B07BE6`, hue 270 to 330 only); *shape* (discrete, tapering, forking strands rooted at a hull seam or panel gap, crossing the rim **radially**, versus **one continuous band tangent to the rim** with no root and no end); *motion* (beat-locked, stepped, irregular on the hash schedule, versus smooth breathing with a 4 to 6 s period that Sanity must not lock to `tell_beat`); *scope* (global, every client and every lit region on the same beat, versus per viewer); *falsifiability* (a saner crewmate's screen shows the same strands and no fringe on your rim; strands are drawn only inside the **true** lit region, never in a falsely extended reach). Co-occurrence is legal and must stay legible. If Sanity's fringe cannot satisfy "tangential, continuous, breathing, per-viewer", that is a CONFLICT for the creative-director, not something to resolve by dimming the tell (AC-AT-109).

**Shared vocabulary with Horror's hidden-entity footprints (ART 5.5 (i), (ii)).** The tell and a hidden entity both "move dust and twitch filaments". They differ by *scope and sync* (tell: global, every lit region on the same beat, rising across the window; footprint: local, unsynchronised, spatially coherent along a path, available in any phase including CALM), by *assets and gesture* (footprints use resident web pieces and the dust particle process, never `web_strand_*`, never the band-slide) and by *dust* (tell dust impulses take a direction from a hash of `(epoch, beat, cone)`: uncorrelated between cones and **never toward the locus**, because the visual tell is global and beat-locked, so a direction would make it read as a footprint's spatially coherent path; *where* is the positional audio layer's and STRESS's job). A tell and a footprint may coincide; the test a careful player already uses is "is it everywhere on the beat, or one place?"

### Palette rules

**Allowed:** the web iridescence band (ART 4.1: teal, green, bronze, gold, low saturation) applied as a specular **band**, never a fill, over a Void Steel `#0B1018` mass ("dark base, bright band, nothing between", ART 9.4); **Alarm Red `#E0102A`** on the beacon lens and sweep only, supplied by Emitter State and Lighting (no strand, dust or rim is ever red); Tungsten only as the beam that happens to catch dust and strands; Chemlight `#86B896` may light strands (a glowstick is a real emitter) and adds no Attention colour. **Forbidden:** violet (hue 270 to 330) and flesh (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 percent, V 45 to 85 percent) in any Attention pixel; any loop hue (O2 Cyan, Power Amber, Coolant Ice, Fuel Ember) on a strand; Hull Grey as a strand colour; any new palette entry. **Luminance ladder (ART 4.5):** the beam core stays the brightest thing in frame (ART 3.5); the band's peak is capped at the Hull Grey tier (`BAND_PEAK_LSTAR_MAX` about L* 61, an art hypothesis to confirm in the lighting spike) and sits under the glow threshold so the band **cannot bloom** (ART 9.4); the rim therefore sits below Power Amber and O2 Cyan and below the chemlight, so the watching web out-shouts no loop hue or glowstick. **Depth never changes hue** (ART 4.0, 4.3): the ring raises baseline band intensity only. If a red-lit band drifts toward the flesh hue band, lower the band's saturation under the beacon, never shift its hue. Sampling tests run on engine screenshots at target scale, never on source PNGs (AC-AT-45).

### Audio requirements (AUD-n; Adaptive Audio & Tells owns every sound)

**AUD-1. No cue without a published fact.** Every Attention-owned audio event derives from a state delta in `attention.view`, never from a timer in Audio: onset (`phase` CALM to TELL), beat (`(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` increases), release (TELL to CALM with `tell_outcome` ABORTED), breach-enter (TELL to BREACH), breach pulse (the alarm cadence, M1), subside (BREACH to SUBSIDING) and the pre-tell ambient creak (`level_pm` in quantised CALM bands, never counted by `TellAudit`). Event ids and asset names are Audio's (the audio-director proposes the family token `attn`, e.g. `sfx_attn_tell_onset_01`).

**AUD-2. Audio first.** Beat 0 is published in the same `attention.step` run that enters TELL; the presenter may not render its first resolve for that epoch before it. Both read the same P5 notification, so "audio no later than visual" holds by construction (ART 7.7 HR-9; AC-AT-24).

**AUD-3. The beat schedule is Attention's and is in Formulas (F-12);** this section adds only the audio use: the dedupe key is the pair `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` compared lexicographically (a new tell resets `tell_beat` to 0 while `tell_epoch` increases, so comparing `tell_beat` alone would drop the next onset); at most one beat per poll; no catch-up burst after a hitch, a pause or a restore (AC-AT-115).

**AUD-4. Two layers, one of them contractual.** (a) A positional creak layer anchored at `locus_section_id` (Audio derives the hull anchor from Layout; Attention publishes no coordinates); the positional hull-stress layer at the locus is present from tier 2, and the non-positional bed alone carries tier 1 (Audio AA-17, AA-18). (b) A **non-positional structure-borne bed audible to every living player**, never attenuated by distance to the locus. The bed's existence and listener scope are contractual (AT-15: "perceivable" is defined relative to every living player, which is what makes a tell at an unoccupied lit derelict fair); its audibility margin in dB is Audio's (M2).

**AUD-5. Minimum audible evidence in the floor.** Every legal un-aborted `(tell_len, seed)` publishes at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` (4) beats in the first `TELL_MIN_TICKS` (the placeholders give at least 5); each channel is sufficient alone (ART:954, AC-AT-20, AC-AT-47).

**AUD-6. The abort is audible.** On an abort the bed decays over the release length and beats stop; the player must hear the web lose interest or restraint is never learned. A restore into CALM with `tell_outcome` ABORTED plays nothing (transitions only).

**AUD-7. Truth-channel audio.** Tell and breach audio are never filtered, delayed, muted, doubled or synthesised by the Perception Filter or Sanity. *Can audio lie on this channel? Never, at any tier (AT-17).* Prohibited: a false calm (cues suppressed during a real tell) and a phantom tell. Sanity's audio uses a family disjoint from the tell's, and from the Graph's loop sounds and Horror's own sub-tell (identity table and lint are Audio's, M6).

**AUD-8. Quantised pre-tell cues; no music change before beat 0.** Ordinary hull-settling creaks, floor-gated by `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM` and stepped by `CUE_STEPS`; no continuous mapping, no pitched rising tone, no drone. Adaptive music must not telegraph the tell earlier than beat 0 (must-repeat to Audio, not an Attention rule).

**AUD-9. Audio never feeds the sim.** No symbol of `AudioServer`, `AudioStreamPlayer*` or an audio bus appears in Attention's source; footsteps enter only as the Controller's pushed gait class; muting the SFX bus changes nothing in `state_hash` (AC-AT-09). Consequence: the accessibility cost of muting is the user's accepted one (ART:954).

**AUD-10. Breach and subsiding facts Audio must handle.** STRESS outlives SUBSIDING (the Graph's own hold), so `phase == CALM` must never be mapped to "all clear"; the Graph's failing-loop sounds carry the ongoing wrongness. The single beacon is a home-ship fact: a listener on a derelict, a dead home-ship (`NOT_LIVE`) and the T+1 lag leave the visual beacon absent or late, so there audio carries the breach alone. Under reduced flashing the beacon becomes a static mark (LO-26). LO-26 also names a HUD glyph, which contradicts ART:741 and is routed as C27, so the static mark and the audio carry the breach.

**Must-repeat requests to Adaptive Audio & Tells (not Attention rules; the GDD must repeat each or surface a CONFLICT; AC-AT-78).**

| # | Request | Content |
|---|---|---|
| M1 | Alarm cadence | One alarm pulse per `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` (cited, not restated). When the home-ship beacon is live and the listener is on the home-ship's body, align to the beacon's sweep reference (`beacon_aim` is a pure function of `T`); otherwise use the fallback `(T − breach_enter_T) mod BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`. Reduce-flashing never reduces audio |
| M2 | Bed audibility margin | The bed is at least `TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB` (6 dB, Audio's placeholder) above the loudest concurrent non-critical bed in the worst allowed concurrent state (running plus a TOOL-class channel). Not headless-testable: a listening test with a retained capture |
| M3 | Mix priority | (1) Graph consequence cues and the player's own footsteps (the only noise readout, PCI:135); (2) tell beats and the breach alarm; (3) the positional hull-stress layer; (4) ambient and pre-tell cues; (5) music. Alarm duty capped at `ALARM_DUTY_MAX_PM` of each sweep. Beds duck under tell beats; beats never duck under music |
| M4 | Breach-entry loudness bound | The loudness step from the last tell beat to the breach-enter event is at most `BREACH_ENTRY_STEP_LU_MAX` (8 LU, placeholder): the tell precedes it, so it must not become a jump scare |
| M5 | Reserved voice | One reserved voice for tell and alarm cues; lower priorities drop first (-> ADR candidate) |
| M6 | Disjoint families | An identity table and a lint comparing asset-tag families |

*Rejected for MVP.* The "held breath" silence before breach (it would reduce evidence inside the floor window) and a caption for tell beats (a caption is an Attention indicator in effect; routed to the accessibility-specialist and the user, OQ-AT-1).

### Art-bible amendments proposed (wording only, routed; none applied)

1. **ART 2.7, 2.1, 7.3, 7.6 item 9 (C1).** The "needle that drifts on a *real* gauge" leaves the Tell: no Graph or instrument value may take Attention as input (LSG:281) and a needle with no physical cause would be a lie (FT-4). Replace with "Creaking (heard, structure-borne; the visible Tell is the rim: filaments, dust, and a gutter on lamps). The Tell never moves a needle: a needle moves only when the ship's loop moves"; the tremor stays an honest signature whose cause is real STRESS damage, not Attention.
2. **ART 2.7 (hairlines).** Add: "Hull-stress hairlines belong to the Integrity trail (3.3) and to hidden-entity footprints (5.5 (v)); they are not part of the Tell."
3. **ART 2.7, 8.3, 8.13 (LO:1172).** Fixtures "never flicker" gains two documented gutter occasions: the cryo-bay lamp's idle gutter (2.2) and the Tell's gutter on non-emergency fixtures and node lanterns (2.7). "Flicker" remains the deployable's word. This is Lighting's Open Question 1 (routed with Attention as its next-GDD target): add "fixtures and node lanterns may gutter at the Attention tell", and "the one exception" becomes two.
4. **ART 2.8.** Add: "The alarm beacon is a single home-ship fixture. On a derelict the breach is carried by the failure trails, the held rim of 2.7's strands and the audio; no red source is added there (Alarm Red stays rare, 4.1)."
5. **ART 2.7, carried element.** Add the exclusivity and non-emission clauses of AV-15 and AV-18.

### Asset implications (specifications, no pixels)

All names follow ART 8.5 (`<cat>[_<dialect>]_<subject>[_<variant tokens>]_<map>.png`; the sheet is the file unit; states and poses are Aseprite tags and slices). Every sheet carries at least 4 px transparent padding on non-tile cells (ART 8.6, 8.11), lossless, binary alpha, palette-locked to ART 4. Cost rule (ART 2.0): **one authored pose pair per strand piece, no sprite set per state.**

| # | Asset | Stem and maps | Size and frames | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Tell strand pieces (4 shapes) | `web_strand_01` to `_04`, each with `_alb`, `_nrm`, `_spc`, `_msk` | 96x96 cells; poses `held` and `taut` | Branching, tapering (trunk at least 2 px, tip 1 px), no right angles, no closed circles. `_msk` G = root-to-tip ramp driving the stepped resolve and the band-slide (stub = the first about 40 percent); `_msk` B = the iridescence-band region. `stub`, `resolve`, `twitch`, `lean`, `release` are shader or animator states of the two poses, not extra frames |
| A2 | Long strand pieces (tier 3; cuttable) | `web_strand_05`, `_06` | 144x144, `held` and `taut` | MVP may ship A1 only |
| A3 | Root and tip caps | `web_strand_root_01`, `web_strand_tip_01` | 48x48 | Roots read as emerging from behind a panel or through a seam (ART 3.3) |
| A4 | Resolve strip (optional; cuttable) | `vfx_tell_resolve_alb.png` | 48x48, 4 to 6 frames | Only if the mask-driven reveal cannot carry the band-slide |
| A5 | Web-root sites | slices `web_root_*` inside the room-kit atlases | slice data only | Every wall-base, seam and panel-gap junction exposes a root slice so the presenter has deterministic sites (a kit requirement, flagged AF-6) |
| A6 | Beacon housing, `off` and `on` frames | `env_shared_beacon_housing` | one cell, 2 frames | The lit Alarm Red lens legible with the sweep removed is the reduce-flashing static mark (LO-26). Owner: Derelict Authoring / environment kit (DA-24), not Attention (flagged AF-4) |
| A7 | Dust impulse | none new | reuses the 4x4 and 8x8 dust textures (ART 8.3) | Behaviour only: a short velocity impulse on existing particles |
| A8-A9 | Gutter; light textures | none | light parameter only; beacon texture exists | Lighting's recipe |
| A10 | HUD and UI | **none, by decision** | n/a | No Attention indicator anywhere (ART 7.6 item 15; ART:741) |

`web_strand_*` and `vfx_tell_*` stems are reserved for the Attention Presenter (AV-18). No new material beyond the per-segment iridescence `ShaderMaterial` already counted (ART 8.10, at most 5 materials), no new light; strands add draw calls only on the Y-sorted layer (PIPE row 16); lines are sprites, never runtime line draws (ART 8.4).

### Presenter values (art values, `[PLACEHOLDER]`, `presentation.json`; not simulation constants)

| Value | Default | Meaning and owner |
|---|---|---|
| `STRAND_RESOLVE_FRAMES` | 4 (12 fps stepped = 20 ticks, 333 ms) | the resolve and band-slide length (ART) |
| `STUB_REVEAL_FRAMES` | 3 (15 ticks, 250 ms) | the pre-tell stub step reveal (ART) |
| `TAUT_SNAP_FRAMES` | 2 (10 ticks, 167 ms) | the BREACH-entry snap (ART) |
| `STRAND_RELEASE_TICKS` | equal to Audio's `RELEASE_TICKS` (90, 1.5 s) | the abort release; equality is the requirement (ART/AD) |
| SUBSIDING retraction | spread over `SUBSIDE_TICKS` | slack then stepped decrements (ART) |
| `STUB_MAX_PM`, `DENSITY_BASE_PM`, `DENSITY_STEP_PM`, `DENSITY_MAX_PM` | 200, 300, 30, 800 permille per site | seeds for the lighting spike; the constraint is `STUB_MAX_PM < DENSITY_BASE_PM`, tuned until the expected count in a typical cone matches `FILAMENT_STRANDS[tier]` (ART/SD) |
| `STRAND_VIEW_CAP` | 9 per lit region (overlapping regions: a site counts once; a screen-wide ceiling is routed to the art-director as a producer-scope fallback) | the hard ceiling (ART 3.5 supporting shape) |
| dust impulse | 1x / 2x / 3x a base unit at tiers 1 / 2 / 3 | direction from a hash of `(epoch, beat, cone)`, never toward the locus (ART) |
| `RIM_BAND_PM` | 350 of the emitter's reach (the cone length for a BEAM, the pool radius for FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN and DEPLOYABLE; operative since the gate widened strands to lit regions) | the band in which the guaranteed resolve looks for the nearest-to-rim site (spike-confirmed; ART) |
| `BAND_PEAK_LSTAR_MAX` | about L* 61 | the band-peak ceiling, spike-confirmed (ART) |

### Accessibility and reduced-effect behaviour

- **Reduce-motion (AT-24; HR-8).** Resolve is a single frame (no band-slide); twitch and sway are removed; dust impulses fall to the tier-1 amplitude and stop escalating; **the density step per beat is kept** (the backbone), so "more filaments" survives; stub reveals become one frame. The held rim is unchanged.
- **Reduce-flashing (LO-26; HR-13).** The gutter keeps its spacing (Lighting). The beacon becomes the static lit lens (A6 `on`), with no sweep and no pulse. LO-26 also names a HUD glyph, which contradicts ART:741 and is routed as C27, so the static mark and the audio carry the breach; no glyph is drawn.
- **Colourblind (ART 4.7).** Strands are identified by shape, taper, fork and beat-locked motion; no hue-only channel. The beacon by rotation and lens shape.
- **Low vision (HR-15).** The ambient-floor option reveals hull only, never strands; audio-first beat 0 matters most for this player.
- **Deaf or hard of hearing.** The visual rim is the only channel (ART 7.6 item 15), which is why AV-7 guarantees a resolve per beat per lit region; with the chest lamp off but fixtures, lanterns or glowsticks lit the pools still carry it (gate ruling, OQ-AT-3 closed), and with nothing lit there is none (the accepted cost, `ART:954`).
- **No captions and no indicator of any kind** under any setting (AT-24).
- **Photosensitivity.** The 3 Hz world ceiling (AV-3); the accessibility-specialist confirms.

### Engine feasibility notes

The engine reference (`docs/engine-reference/godot/`) has an `audio.md` and a `rendering.md`; it has **no scripting or core file**, so integer-division behaviour and `Dictionary` ordering are verified by the AC-AT-54a spike. Verified from `audio.md`: `AudioStreamPlayer2D` `area_mask` defaults to 0 in 4.7 and the installed editor is 4.7.2, so any Area2D-based `audio_bus_override` (per-section muffle) does nothing until the mask is set explicitly; `AudioEffectSpectrumAnalyzer.tap_back_pos` is removed (do not use a spectrum analyser as a test probe); beats trigger from published sim state via the presentation subscriber, never from `_process` wall time. **Not in the reference (-> spike or ADR):** whether a sprite shader can know its distance to a light's edge (rim-band modulation; the fallback is the AV-7 hash plus the nearest-to-rim guaranteed resolve, with resolved sites persisting for the epoch), which camera defines the listener for 2D positional audio inside the 640x360 world SubViewport under the integer zoom ladder (the non-positional bed does not depend on it), AudioServer output latency on Windows, and the band-peak and glow-threshold figures (the lighting spike, ART 8.12). 4.7 typed-return overrides apply to any audio-facing `read_view()`: an explicit return on every path. -> ADR candidates: presentation-subscriber polling and voice pooling; the integer hash shared with Lighting (LO-19); the SubViewport listener policy; the reserved-voice policy.

*Flags.* **AF-1** a derelict breach has a thin visual signal (the single beacon is not in view; the held rim, the trails and the audio carry it; if playtests show it is not legible the options are a design call, not an art one, and neither a second beacon nor a red tint is available inside DA-24 and ART 4.1; OQ-AT-4). **AF-3** rim-band modulation and per-light edge distance are unmeasured. **AF-4** the beacon housing's static mark has no owner yet. **AF-6** and **AF-9** are scope additions awaiting the producer (`web_root_*` slices in room kits; up to six new strand sheets, of which A2 and A4 are cuttable; A1 carries the standard height pass, about +20 to 30 percent on those sheets).

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:attention-system` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions, and generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

*Scope note.* Attention has no verb of its own: the player never "operates" it, so the feel being specified is the **world's responsiveness to the player's emission** and the **weight of the tell**. Latencies below are measured at `TICK_RATE` 60 (one tick = 16.7 ms; the step hook runs every 6 ticks = 100 ms; presenter frames at 12 fps stepped = 5 ticks = 83 ms per frame). Sim numbers are the GDD's placeholders; presenter numbers are the art values of Visual/Audio Requirements. Audio latencies are Adaptive Audio's and AudioServer's, not Attention's, and are named as such.

### Feel Reference

*Borrowed:* Alien: Isolation's vent bang before the drop (audio first, the threat announced by the building) and Subnautica's distant leviathan call (heard everywhere, located nowhere): the tell is a bed you cannot localise and a beat you cannot count down. Pandorum's chest-lamp dread: the light never changes, the world at its edge does. *Anti-reference:* Resident Evil 7 and Outlast (heartbeat, breath, screen shake, tunnel vision) and Darkest Dungeon's stress bar: "the ship changes, you don't" (PCI:135, PIPE:151). Nothing about the player's body, view or controls reacts; if a tester says "my character panicked", the feel is wrong.

### Input Responsiveness

No player input is bound to Attention. The table is therefore *act to world response*: how soon the web's published state, and then the world, answers something the player did. The deliberate rule is that the **level responds fast and the world responds late and in steps** — the integrator reacts within one run, the presentation does not mirror it frame by frame, because a mirror would be a meter.

| Player act | Max act-to-response latency | Tick budget | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Run, cut or throw pushes noise | integrated in the next run: at most 100 ms (6 ticks); a push landing after the hook in the same tick integrates the run after (at most 200 ms) | 6 to 12 | the Controller's per-tick push has a zero- or one-tick lag (BDY-19), below the run resolution |
| Crossing `ATT_THRESHOLD` to TELL entry and beat 0 published | the same run that integrates the crossing | 0 additional | no instant-breach path exists; beat 0 is audio-first (AUD-2) |
| Beat 0 published to the first visible strand stirring | one render frame to poll (at most 17 ms) plus the first stepped frame (at most 83 ms): at most 100 ms | 6 | the resolve then completes over `STRAND_RESOLVE_FRAMES` (333 ms); audio output latency is AudioServer's, not counted here |
| Last eligible emitter goes dark (chest lamp off and no fixture, lantern or glowstick lit) to every filament gone | at most 2 ticks (33 ms): the T+1 commit of the switch-off (`toggle_beam`, EM-7, or the fixture or lantern verb) plus one render frame | 2 | unlit strands are not drawn (Principle 1); the level itself still decays on its own memory |
| Chest lamp off, still, to the level falling under `theta_HOLD` (Moment A, ring 1) | 480 ticks = 8.0 s | 480 | a design target, not a latency to minimise (F-15b) |
| All inputs zero to the abort completing | 594 ticks = 9.9 s (80 runs to leave `theta_HOLD` plus the 20-run hold) | 594 | the early-abort path; the at-due path ends at `breach_due_T` |
| BREACH entry to the beacon lens lit | 1 tick (17 ms, the T+1 SYSTEM command) | 1 | `max_age_ticks` 120 is slack, not latency |
| BREACH entry to the rim held taut | the same frame as the phase change plus `TAUT_SNAP_FRAMES` (167 ms) | 10 | the beacon, the snap and the STRESS trail changes land within about 0.2 s of each other |
| Pod-hop departure from a non-home locus to BREACH ending | at most one run (100 ms) after the last occupant's `actor_section` leaves `locus_body` | 6 | provisional on Web Map |

### Animation Feel Targets

Startup, active and recovery are in stepped presenter frames (12 fps). "Committed" = the animation cannot be interrupted by a later event except where noted.

| Animation | Startup frames | Active frames | Recovery frames | Feel goal | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stub reveal (pre-tell step) | 0 | 3 (250 ms) | 0 | a held-breath twitch of the corner of the eye; never a pop | static after; hysteresis `CUE_HYST_PM` prevents chatter at an edge |
| Strand resolve with band-slide (a beat) | 0 | 4 (333 ms) | 0 | a deliberate unfurl, one slide root to tip | the only motion that means "beat"; at most 2 per second per cone |
| Twitch (tier 2) | 0 | 1 (83 ms) | 0 | a nervous tip offset of 1 art px | at most once per two beats per strand; removed under reduce-motion |
| Sway (tier 3) | 0 | 2 (167 ms) per pose change | 0 | a slow lean between two poses | at most 1 Hz; removed under reduce-motion |
| Dust impulse | 0 | one nearest-filtered deflection per beat | the dust's own drift | air displaced by something unseen | amplitude 1x / 2x / 3x by tier |
| Snap-taut (BREACH entry) | 0 | 2 (167 ms) | then absolutely still | a drawn breath, then stillness | the only fast Attention motion; reads as "it has decided" |
| Release (abort) | 0 | `STRAND_RELEASE_TICKS` (90 ticks, 1.5 s) | 0 | the web losing interest: slow, quiet, no band-slide | equal in length to Audio's release cue |
| Subside retraction | 0 | spread over `SUBSIDE_TICKS` (1,200 ticks, 20 s) | 0 | wrongness draining out, not an all-clear | nothing celebratory |
| Beacon sweep (Emitter State's) | 0 | `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` (128 ticks = 2.13 s) per rotation | 0 | a slow red search across the walls | 20 degree cone; never a full-frame flash |

### Impact Moments

| Impact type | Duration | Effect description | Configurable? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hit-stop (freeze frames) | 0 ms | none: a freeze would be a loss of control the player did not choose, and Attention never takes the controls | no (fixed 0; AC-AT-53) |
| Screen shake | 0 ms | none in any phase, including BREACH entry (the trails and the beacon carry the impact) | no (fixed 0; PIPE:151) |
| Camera impact, zoom pulse, FOV change | 0 ms | none | no |
| Controller rumble | none designed | any rumble would be a Controller or accessibility decision and may not be keyed to the level or the tell | n/a (OQ-AT-1 covers the indicator question) |
| Time-scale slowdown | 0 ms | none: sim `T` never scales for Attention | no |
| Onset (beat 0) | one resolve (333 ms) plus the first dust impulse plus Audio's onset event | the moment the player learns "it noticed"; the unique `tell_beat == 0` signature | strand and dust values yes (art); the beat-0 rule no |
| Breach entry | about 200 ms: the snap (167 ms) plus the beacon at T+1 plus the STRESS trail changes | the loudest moment the system produces, bounded in loudness (M4) and never a jump scare because a tell preceded it | art values yes; the T+1 command no |
| Abort | 1.5 s release | a quiet exhale | `RELEASE_TICKS` is Audio's |

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight.** Heavy and deliberate. Nothing in the tell is fast except the snap: the beat interval runs from 150 ticks (2.5 s) down to 45 ticks (0.75 s) over 1,800 ticks, with at least 30 ticks (0.5 s) between beats, so the escalation is felt as a closing-in, not a flutter. A tell lasts 15 to 30 s in play (the floor of 10 s only through the arming seam).
- **Player control.** High control throughout. The lamp, the camera, the speed (`V_WALK` 2, `V_RUN` 3 px per tick) and the controls are byte-identical in every phase; the player is never made less able. What is high is the *agency over the cause*: every act that raised the level is one the player can stop.
- **Snap quality.** The sim is analog (an integrating level with a long memory); the presentation is binary and stepped (quantised cue steps, 12 fps resolves). The player never reads a smooth quantity.
- **Acceleration model.** Simulation feel: the level ramps with the player's behaviour and decays on a memory of `TAU_ATT_TICKS` (1,800 ticks = 30 s), so a tell is the result of the last half-minute, never of one frame. Entry is gated, not instant.
- **Failure texture.** Fair and legible. A breach follows a tell the player could have perceived and, by going dark, aborted; the read on why they failed is "we heard it creaking and kept cutting" (Moment B). A tell that cannot be beaten, or a breach with no tell, is a defect by contract (AC-AT-01, AC-AT-16).

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

Playtest-verifiable without instruments; the quantitative metrics are AC-AT-80.

- [ ] In at least 6 of 8 recorded sessions a tester, unprompted, describes the onset as the web or the ship "noticing", "listening" or "starting" (not as a timer or a jump scare).
- [ ] No tester reports that the chest lamp, the camera, the controls or the walking speed changed during a tell or a breach (AC-AT-53 is the automated half).
- [ ] After a breach, at least 6 of 8 testers can name at least one act of their own (a lamp, a cut, a run, a breaker) that raised it, without a prompt.
- [ ] At least 6 of 8 testers report that a breach cost them something (a failed system, a lost item or time, a narrow escape, a death) and not that "nothing happened"; fewer than 6 means commit has become dominant (AT-11, the Moment-ordering guard).
- [ ] No tester reads any on-screen element as an attention indicator; no reviewer describes a filament count as "filling up" in the pre-tell cues (AC-AT-49c is the automated half).
- [ ] The abort reads as "it lost interest", not as "a success jingle": no tester reports the release as a reward cue (AC-AT-111).
- [ ] The beacon, the snap and the trail changes at breach entry read as one event; testers describe a derelict breach (no beacon in view) as legible through the held rim, the trails and the audio (flag AF-1; OQ-AT-4 if not).
- [ ] No reviewer uses the words "floaty", "laggy" or "unresponsive" about the tell's onset, and none reports a catch-up burst of beats after a hitch or a load (AC-AT-115).
- [ ] The tell never feels like a timer: testers cannot predict the breach moment from the cadence (the escalation is elapsed-keyed, AC-AT-81).

## UI Requirements

**No UI of its own, by decision.** The Attention System ships no HUD element, no screen, no menu, no world-space icon, no caption and no instrument, and none is permitted under any accessibility setting: the art bible forbids an Attention indicator anywhere (`ART:741`, 7.6 item 15 at `ART:954`), Pillar 3 says attention is "shown in the world ... never as a hidden meter", and Pillar 1 makes the pipe the player reads, not a number. There is accordingly **no UX Flag** (`/ux-design` has nothing to author for this system) and no per-screen UI evidence is owed; AC-AT-66 (scan plus review checklist) stands in for the UI gate. The table below lists the information a UI could carry, who owns the surface that does carry it, and what Attention publishes for it.

| Information | Display location | Update frequency | Condition | Owner and note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| The attention level (`level_pm`, `L`) | **Nowhere in release builds.** Quantised, floor-gated, diegetic density only (pre-tell stubs and creaks, AT-26) | the `CUE_STEPS` edges only | CALM, above `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM` | Presenter and Audio; no continuous mapping to any visible or audible parameter (AC-AT-49c) |
| Tell in progress, time left, beat count, tier | **Nowhere.** The world carries it: filaments, dust, the bed, the lamp gutter | per published beat | TELL | `tell_remaining` is read only by Save/Load, SIM readers and the DIAGNOSTIC Debug Overlay; never by a PRESENTATION subscriber (AC-AT-39) |
| Breach in progress | the home-ship beacon (a world object) and the Graph's own consequence surfaces (trails, instruments reading real loops) | the beacon's sweep, `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` | BREACH | Emitter State and Failure & Trail; In-World Instruments show a loop's real value, never Attention |
| Tell, breach and abort captions | **None.** A caption is an Attention indicator in effect | n/a | n/a | routed to the accessibility-specialist and the user (OQ-AT-1); no caption field exists (AC-AT-103) |
| Accessibility settings that touch this system | the settings screen: **Reduce motion** and **Reduce flashing** (and the existing low-vision floor) | on change | always | owned by the settings and accessibility documents (`design/accessibility-requirements.md`); this GDD defines only what each does to Attention's visuals (Visual/Audio Requirements). No setting displays or sonifies the level |
| Debug readout of `L`, `level_pm`, the ledger, `tell_remaining`, `TellAudit`, the diagnostic counters | the **Debug Overlay** and the diagnostics channel only (designed, `debug-overlay-true-values.md`) | per run, development builds | development only | the one place values may be shown; the overlay is absent from release builds (AC-AT-66); its own UI evidence is the overlay GDD's |
| Save indicator and autosave gating | none from Attention | n/a | n/a | Save/Load owns the gate; Attention publishes only `tell_active`, `tell_remaining` and `breach_phase` (AT-16) |

*Stored for display?* Nothing. "Attention restores exactly; no meter is stored for display" (AT-27). *Neighbouring UI that must not absorb Attention:* HUD & Suit Layer (the chest-lamp state and suit vitals are the player's own, not the web's), In-World Instruments (a needle moves only when the ship's loop moves; the Tell never moves one, C1), Chart Table (depth rings are shown as place, not as attention). Each of those GDDs must repeat this rule or surface a CONFLICT (AC-AT-61, AC-AT-66).

#### Registry obligations (written from the final text only; Phase 5)

*New entries, each `[PLACEHOLDER]` unless derived (written from the final text, R28 and CP-C10):* **block, hook, view, mutator, command:** `attention.state`, `attention.step`, `attention.view`, `push_stimulus`, `attention_arm_tell`. **Intake and level:** `U_TOOL`, `U_LOUD`, `TAU_ATT_TICKS`, `ATT_THRESHOLD`, `HOLD_PM`, `L_CEIL_PM`, `ATT_MARGIN`, `W_L[kind]` (the per-kind light weights), `W_P`, `W_S`, `B_table`, `DARK_FLOOR_LOAD_W`. **Tell:** `TELL_DRAW_LO`, `TELL_DRAW_HI`, `TELL_MAX_TICKS`, `TELL_REARM_TICKS`, `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS`, `ABORT_RELIEF_PM`, `REACT_ALLOW_TICKS`; `TELL_MIN_TICKS` is registered as **derived** (`ticks_for_seconds(W_MIN_WARNING_S)`), not as a constant of this GDD. **Beats and tier:** `BEAT_START_TICKS`, `BEAT_END_TICKS`, `BEAT_RAMP_TICKS`, `BEAT_JITTER_TICKS`, `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS`, `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR`, `TIER2_TICKS`, `TIER3_TICKS`, `HASH_SEED`. **Breach and after:** `BREACH_MIN_TICKS`, `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, `CLEAR_HOLD_TICKS`, `SUBSIDE_TICKS`, `REFRACT_TICKS`, `POST_PM`, `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS`, `STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS`, `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`, `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS`, `LOCUS_OCC_PREF_PM`. **Presentation cues:** `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM`, `CUE_STEPS`, `CUE_HYST_PM`, `CUE_MARGIN_PM`. *New formulas:* `attention_integrator`, `tell_window_contract`, `noise_unit_relations` (IR-1 to IR-3), `abortability_invariant`, `abortability_play`, `event_defer_invariant`, `survivable_dark_floor`. `G_LO` and `G_HI` are **not** registry entries and receive no `referenced_by` (C24).
*`referenced_by: design/gdd/attention-system.md` is added to every pending entry:* `MOD_FLOOR_PM`, `DIP_DEPTH_PM`, `power_allocation`, `autosave_due`, `autosave_gate`, `modifier_stack`, `gutter_recipe`, `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT`, `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS`, `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS`; *and added to:* `NOISE_PER_TICK`, `noise_push`, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`, `emitter_kind`, `illumination`, `beacon_aim`, `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`, `W_MIN_WARNING_S`, `MAX_EMITTERS`, `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`, `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`, `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`, `MAX_FAULT_GLOW`, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`, `STEP_BUDGET_MS`, `TICK_RATE`, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`. `INRUSH_PM` and `INRUSH_STEPS` are registered Graph constants (cited by name).
