| C16 | The brief treated Ship Body and Derelict Authoring as on disk; at ruling time they were in flight; **both are now on disk with status In Design** and both are still being rewritten (Body's lines moved between 21:04, 21:10 and 21:21) | `BDY:67`, `:340`, `:388`, `:494` (brief); `DA-23`, `DA-24` | BDY and DA citations carry the tag "sibling in flight", are given by section id first, and are re-grepped at the confirming scan; DA is cited by DA-23 and DA-24, no longer by the rulings file |
| C17 | The brief's progress-keyed cadence for escalation | brief D11(a) (`tell_progress_pm`) | superseded: elapsed-keyed (AT-9); `tell_progress_pm` not published |
| C18 | (author stage) Ruling-time wording "Controller pushes from the previous tick's `moved`" vs Ship Body 4.1, which rejects a persisted `moved` | BDY refresh row "Controller / Attention" (`option (a), persisting`; `BDY:988` at 23:14, `:985` at 21:21); rulings R3 | Attention repeats Body's text and accepts either timing (AT-3); the Controller owner chooses |
| C19 | (author stage) The rulings' one-way read list omits the Controller's `loco_state`, which "living player" and `IN_POD` need | rulings R26; `PCI:171-188` | read added to AT-25 as AT-31; no cycle (PCI:1010) |
| C20 | (part 2) DA-24 says "Attention … may relocate it"; placement is an Authoring data row, not an Attention power | `DA-24` (`may relocate it`) | refresh owed to DA: "Attention only submits `set_beacon` to the one beacon the data holds" (AT-18); not absorbed here |
| C21 | (part 2) The `gates_actions` value for BEACON: the registry row says "provisional, user decision pending" and EM:200 routes it to the technical-director and game-designer | `EM:200`; registry `emitter_kind` | Attention concurs with `false` and does not decide (AT-18); `max_age_ticks` 120 stays confirmed |
| C22 | (part 2; refreshed for Horror HE-28, C-HE-3) Footprint budget: LSG:807 honours the first `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` in arrival order within P2, but Horror deposits by path (a) (Class B `deposit_trace` and `apply_suit_damage` from the `horror.step` hook, no SYSTEM command), so the P2 premise does not hold; Attention claims **no** first place; the registry unit (revised 2026-10-27) says per run across all callers and subjects (global), the first 16 by arrival applied in ascending subject id, the rest dropped and counted, which matches LSG:727 | `LSG:807`, `LSG:727`; registry `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` | Horror must-repeat: its per-run footprint budget is at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP − STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`; the wording is resolved in the registry; a dropped STRESS deposit is re-armed by the next burst (edge cases) |
| C23 | (part 2) The cue floor `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM` 350 failed AT-26's own calibration lint (406 pm at ring 1, about 425 pm at ring 3) | AT-26; Formulas F-24, F-25 | superseded: the default is derived (550) and 350 is recorded as the superseded seed (Tuning Knobs) |
| C24 | (part 2) `G_LO`/`G_HI` were listed as locked constants; they are variables of Lighting's `gutter_recipe` formula (REG:2187-2188 (registry `gutter_recipe` variables), tier 1 480/960 frames), not registry entries | registry `gutter_recipe`; `LO:257` | cited as "Lighting's `gutter_recipe` bounds"; removed from the locked-constant list; no `referenced_by` is added to them |
| C25 | (Visual/Audio pass) The earlier ruling (R20) drew filaments "only inside a lit cone"; the art-director proposes "any lit region" (a fixture pool, lantern, glowstick pool, pod lights) so a deaf player in a fixture-lit home-ship has a visual channel | AT-20 (`lit cone`); Visual/Audio Requirements AV-14; `ART:954` | RESOLVED at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate: widened to the lit region of BEAM, FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN and DEPLOYABLE (AT-20, AV-7, AV-14 and AC-AT-44 amended); cone-only is a producer scope fallback only |
| C26 | (Gate CD-GDD-ALIGN) Cross-system obligations created by the applied concerns: Depth Rings & Scarcity must satisfy the per-ring light and power stimulus gain (never noise) or surface a CONFLICT; Derelict Authoring must repeat that every possible locus body on derelicts A and B holds an active overload or fatigue fault; Horror Entities' single MVP behaviour wired to BREACH and a non-lethal presence outside BREACH are blocking | AT-6, AT-11, AT-15; OQ-AT-9, OQ-AT-13; AC-AT-77, AC-AT-117, AC-AT-119 | rows owed to Depth Rings & Scarcity, Derelict Authoring and Horror Entities at their next edit; none applied here |
| C27 | (final review) LO-26 says that under reduce-flashing the beacon becomes "a static mark plus its sound plus a HUD glyph (the HUD's concern)" (also LO:77 and LO:901). A HUD glyph for the breach phase is an Attention indicator, which ART:741 and 7.6 item 15 (ART:954) forbid under every accessibility setting | `LO:285` (`HUD glyph`), `LO:77` (`glyph`), `LO:901` (`HUD glyph`); `ART:741` | Attention keeps "static mark plus audio, no glyph" (AT-18, AT-24, AUD-10, V14). Refresh owed to Lighting: drop "plus a HUD glyph", or route the glyph to the creative-director as an exception to ART:741. Not decided here (OQ-AT-21) |

**Stale-token list for the confirming scan (`consistency-check` follow-up):** `horror.tell` as Attention's stream; `(6, 2)` or `offset 2` for Attention; "Suit pushes" (for Attention); `flicker` near fixture, lantern or tell outside the verbatim pillar quote; `meter`, `bar`, `gauge`, `indicator` near Attention outside a negation or a quotation; `hand point` as the beam origin; "pushed in by the Graph"; `breach_phase` containing TELL; `tel_remaining` typos; `breach_epoch` (renamed `tell_epoch`); `tell_progress_pm` (not published); `AMBIENT_STEPS` (renamed `CUE_STEPS`); "persisted `moved`" as the adopted option; "previous tick's `moved`" as the adopted option.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Source and ids.* Written from Core Rules AT-1 to AT-32, States and Transitions, Formulas F-1 to F-25 and Edge Cases E-1 to E-63. **Ids are stable:** `AC-AT-01` to `AC-AT-61` and `AC-AT-01a` are the ids the Core Rules already cite; `AC-AT-62` to `AC-AT-80` were added by the first draft; `AC-AT-81` and above close the qa-lead's gaps and the art-director's and audio-director's seeds. A criterion the qa-lead asked to split keeps its id with a letter (`06a` to `06e`, `49a` to `49c`, `54a`, `54b`, `57a` to `57g`). The tell-window criteria come first, as the index requires (IDX:34). Every number is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` from this GDD: a test reads the data file, never a literal, except where the number IS the point.

*Tags and gates (rigor full, project testing standard).* **(auto)** headless automated test: Logic or Integration evidence, `tests/unit/attention/` or `tests/integration/attention/`, BLOCKING. **(data-lint)** runs at boot or in a unit test over `assets/data/attention/`; it refuses boot, so it is BLOCKING in CI; every data-lint has a **negative fixture** (a data set that must fail). **(source-lint)** a static scan in `tools/ci/`, with a negative fixture. **(review)** a document or code review at a named point. **(manual)** a retained screenshot or capture in `production/qa/evidence/attention/[ac-id]-[rung|setting]-[date].png` plus an **art-director sign-off line**; Visual/Feel, BLOCKING (a capture that was discarded is an assertion, not evidence). **(playtest)** a playtest report in `production/qa/evidence/`, non-blocking. **(spike)** an engine or cost question answered by a measured spike. **(deferred: blocked on [system])** cannot run until that GDD exists; it stays an open item in the sprint-review blocker list and is never counted as a pass. Attention ships no screen, so no UI screenshot is owed (UI Requirements).

*Fixture glossary (so a tester without the GDD can run it).*

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| `TellAudit` | a DIAGNOSTIC-class ledger recording `(tick, caller, kind, section, tell_epoch, phase)` for every M17 RUPTURE, GOUGE and STRESS and every environmental M18 `apply_damage(..., EXOGENOUS)`; provisionally also every lethal act by a horror on a crew body (AT-15). A test fixture, written before Horror and Audio exist |
| Stub callers | test doubles that call M17 `deposit_trace` and M18 `apply_damage` with a chosen kind, section and tick, and a Horror double that can saturate `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` |
| Data files | `assets/data/attention/{intake, integrator, weights, baseline, tell, breach, presentation}.json` |
| `B_table` | rows ring 1 / 2 / 3 = 300 / 450 / 550 AU (rings 2 and 3 are test rows) |
| Dark fixture | `DARK_FLOOR_LOAD_W` 1,500 W, the EMERGENCY lamp (output 1000, reach 144) and `N_GLOW_DARK` (2, the shipping value under the valve-OPEN fallback; 1 under the closed-valve staging once ASK-G1 is accepted; 0 on the clean ship) |
| Home-ship fixture | three fixtures plus the EMERGENCY lamp, 1,900 W of draw, 766 mAU of light and power per tick |
| Oracle | a test-side arbitrary-precision integer model of F-1 to F-5 (a fixture, never sim code) |
| Roots | `tests/unit/attention/`, `tests/integration/attention/`, `tests/performance/attention/`, `tools/ci/` (source lints) |

### A. The tell-window contract (written first)

- **AC-AT-01 (auto) The contract holds over a recorded run.** GIVEN a recorded run with stub callers for M17 RUPTURE, GOUGE and STRESS and for environmental M18 `apply_damage(..., EXOGENOUS)`, and a `TellAudit` ledger, WHEN the replay evaluates `tell_window_contract` (F-23), THEN every harm event `e` has an epoch `k` with `tell_start_k + tell_len_k <= t_e < subside_end_k`, `tell_len_k >= TELL_MIN_TICKS` and `section_e in locus_body_k`, and the number of violations is 0; a stub that deposits one run early (T 8,238 against a due run 8,244) and a stub that deposits at `subside_end` both fail. GIVEN a locus section cut away mid-BREACH, so that the body falls back per E-27, THEN deposits on the recomputed body pass, a `LOCUS_FALLBACK` row is recorded, and a deposit on the old body after the fallback tick fails. Discharges LSG:1063. [AT-15, F-23]
- **AC-AT-01a (auto) The floor on every path.** GIVEN 10,000 consecutive `rng_counter` values (starting at 0 and at a restored mid-run value) and a stub stream pinned in turn to `TELL_DRAW_LO`, `TELL_DRAW_HI`, `TELL_DRAW_LO - 1` and `TELL_DRAW_HI + 1`, and `attention_arm_tell` arguments 0, 100, 600, 1800 and 9999, WHEN each tell is entered, THEN `tell_len` is in `[TELL_MIN_TICKS, TELL_MAX_TICKS]` for every counter; an armed 100 is clamped to 600 and an armed 9999 to 1800; the out-of-range stub values are refused by the boot lint (they are unreachable in play); and no path reaches BREACH before `tell_start_T + tell_len`. [AT-8, AT-21, F-10]
- **AC-AT-30 (auto) No early breach.** GIVEN any `tell_len`, WHEN the machine runs with `L >= theta_HOLD` throughout, THEN the first BREACH run is the first run with `T >= breach_due_T`, never earlier, in `[breach_due_T, breach_due_T + 5]`; a `LOUD` push at any tick never advances it. [AT-8, F-11]
- **AC-AT-81 (auto) The schedule is elapsed-keyed.** GIVEN two armed tells with `tell_len` 600 and 1800, the same `tell_epoch` number and the same `HASH_SEED`, WHEN each runs un-aborted, THEN the beat offsets, the `tell_beat` count and `tier` for `e` in `[0, 600)` are identical, `tier` is 1 for `e < 600`, 2 for `600 <= e < 1200` and 3 from 1200, and no published field other than `tell_remaining` differs between the two before tick 600. This is the whole reason the end is unreadable. [AT-9, F-12, F-13]
- **AC-AT-88 (auto + data-lint) The tell draw.** GIVEN 10,000 consecutive counters, WHEN organic tells are drawn, THEN every `tell_len` is in `[TELL_DRAW_LO, TELL_DRAW_HI]` (900 to 1800 inclusive), the stream id is `"attention.tell"`, `rng_counter` increases by exactly 1 per organic draw and by 0 for an armed tell, and a stub stream yielding 1,044 at counter 3 gives `tell_len` 1,044. The data-lint requires `TELL_MIN_TICKS <= TELL_DRAW_LO <= TELL_DRAW_HI <= TELL_MAX_TICKS` and `TELL_MIN_TICKS == ticks_for_seconds(W_MIN_WARNING_S)` (a fixture changing `W_MIN_WARNING_S` changes `TELL_MIN_TICKS`; a `TELL_DRAW_LO` below it refuses boot). Negative fixture: `TELL_DRAW_LO` 599. [AT-8, F-10]
- **AC-AT-89 (auto) The predicate.** GIVEN each phase and a section inside and outside `locus_body` (at least eight combinations), WHEN `exogenous_permitted(section)` is read, THEN it is true only in BREACH or SUBSIDING and only inside the body; during the STRESS tail after SUBSIDING (CALM) Attention makes no new call and the predicate is false. [AT-15, States and Transitions, E-34]
- **AC-AT-90 (auto) Player acts are not events.** GIVEN a player cut, a player vent and a crewmate's tool strike (class c friendly harm) in CALM, TELL and BREACH, WHEN `TellAudit` replays, THEN none is recorded and none is a violation. [AT-15, E-56]
- **AC-AT-68 (auto, provisional; deferred: blocked on Horror Entities) Crew-body scope.** GIVEN a Horror double that makes a lethal act on a crew body, WHEN `TellAudit` replays, THEN the act is recorded and satisfies F-23 only inside a told breach's locus body; an act outside it is a violation. Horror Entities must repeat this or surface a CONFLICT. [AT-15, R15]
- **AC-AT-62 (auto) Registration.** GIVEN the kernel's registries, WHEN Attention registers, THEN there is exactly one `SIM` block `attention.state` (`persist` true, `persist_scope WORLD`, `scope SHARED`, `repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY`, `schema_version` 1, `restore_after [graph.state, emitter.state]`) and one hook `attention.step` at (divisor 6, offset 0, `after [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]`); an unknown `after` id fails boot; Suit's `suit.vitals` precedes the hook transitively and no `after: suit.vitals` is declared. [AT-1]

### B. Intake

- **AC-AT-02 (auto) One level.** GIVEN loaded sections A and B and a dormant section C, WHEN pushes arrive with sources in A, B and C, THEN there is one global `L`, ledger entries exist for A and B only, C holds none (LSG:174), and a push attributed to C counts toward `L` but is dropped from the ledger with `dropped_unattributed` incremented. GIVEN a seat whose `actor_section` is unavailable, THEN its noise still enters `I_noise`, the ledger drop is counted and the machine continues. [AT-2, F-2, E-3, E-62]
- **AC-AT-03 (data-lint) IR-1.** GIVEN the intake data, WHEN loaded, THEN `2 <= U_TOOL <= 6`; boot refuses otherwise. Negative fixture: `U_TOOL` 7. [AT-3, F-22]
- **AC-AT-04 (data-lint) IR-2.** GIVEN the intake data, WHEN loaded, THEN `U_LOUD >= 30 * U_TOOL`, and the default 180 satisfies it at `U_TOOL` 2, 4 and 6. Negative fixture: `U_LOUD` 100 at `U_TOOL` 4. [AT-3, F-22]
- **AC-AT-05 (data-lint) IR-3.** GIVEN the integrator data and the cited `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`, WHEN loaded, THEN `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS <= 2 * TAU_ATT_TICKS`. Negative fixture: `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 250. [AT-3, F-22]
- **AC-AT-06a (auto) The intake stub, accrual.** GIVEN an Interaction double (the AC-IA-107 stub, in `tests/integration/interaction/`), WHEN a 180-tick patch accrues, THEN exactly 180 `TOOL` pushes land and `pending` reads 720,000 mAU; a tick that aborts before accrual pushes none. [AT-3, F-1, E-1]
- **AC-AT-06b (auto) Split cuts.** GIVEN a cut split at e = 100 and resumed, THEN the same total is pushed as for one uninterrupted run. [AT-3, F-1]
- **AC-AT-06c (auto) One-shots.** GIVEN cut completion, a seam-cut DONE, a vent apply and a throw landing, THEN each pushes exactly one `LOUD`. [AT-3, IA-33]
- **AC-AT-06d (auto) Silent verbs.** GIVEN a breaker close and a door motor, THEN neither pushes (the inrush enters only through `power_use`). [AT-3, IA-33]
- **AC-AT-06e (auto) QUIET and unknown classes.** GIVEN a `QUIET` push, THEN nothing is added and the `QUIET` counter increments; GIVEN an unknown class or a `kind` other than `NOISE`, THEN the call is a counted no-op and never rejects. [AT-3, E-1, E-2]
- **AC-AT-07 (auto) The beam counts once.** GIVEN a section holding only the owner's beam (1000, 288) and no fixture, WHEN one run executes, THEN `I_light = 1,728` mAU, no `lux_pm` is read and no `probe()` is called. [AT-4, F-3, EM-27]
- **AC-AT-08 (auto + data-lint) Weights that must be zero.** GIVEN a BEACON emitter and a FAULT_GLOW emitter, WHEN a run executes in CALM and in BREACH, THEN the BEACON contributes 0 in both, and the FAULT_GLOW contributes in CALM and TELL and 0 in BREACH and SUBSIDING. The data-lint refuses `W_L[BEACON]` other than 0 (negative fixture 1). [AT-4, F-3]
- **AC-AT-09 (source-lint + auto) No probe, no Audio.** GIVEN the Attention source, WHEN scanned, THEN it contains no `probe()` call and no `AudioServer`, `AudioStreamPlayer*` or audio-bus symbol; and GIVEN a replay with the SFX bus muted, THEN `state_hash` is unchanged at every tick. [AT-4, AT-25, AUD-9]
- **AC-AT-10 (auto) `power_use` absent.** GIVEN a loaded section for which `power_use` returns nothing, WHEN a run executes, THEN `I_power[s]` is 0, `ATT_POWER_ABSENT` is logged once per process and `served_pm` is not substituted. [AT-4, F-4, E-5]
- **AC-AT-63 (auto) Beacon exclusion.** GIVEN BREACH with `draw_w` 2,520, `lamps_lit_w` 600, `beacon_w` 20, `delta_w_last5` +20 and `beacon_step_w` 20, WHEN a run executes, THEN `I_power = 1,140` mAU. GIVEN the control case, phase CALM with the same `draw_w`, `lamps_lit_w` and `delta_w_last5` +20 caused by some other 20 W load (`beacon_w` and `beacon_step_w` are 0 outside BREACH and SUBSIDING), THEN no exclusion applies and `I_power = (100 x 1920 x 6) div 1000 + (6000 x 20 x 6) div 1000` = 1,872, which is also what the BREACH case would reach without the exclusion. GIVEN the switch-off at SUBSIDING entry, THEN the exclusion applies for the five steps after it; GIVEN a beacon commanded on but rejected (`beacon_w` 0), THEN nothing is subtracted. [AT-4, AT-18, F-4, E-31]
- **AC-AT-95 (source-lint) The two doors and the callers.** GIVEN the Attention source, WHEN its outgoing calls are listed, THEN they are only `deposit_trace(STRESS, ...)` and `set_beacon`; no RUPTURE, GOUGE, `apply_damage`, lamp, Lighting or Graph-state write exists; `push_stimulus` refuses or lints out any caller other than the Controller and Interaction (a Horror, Suit or Assembly double is refused; Horror pushes no noise, HE-36); a `kind` other than `NOISE` fails the lint. Negative fixture: a source stub that calls `apply_damage`. [AT-3, AT-4, AT-25, E-1, E-36]
- **AC-AT-96 (integration, auto) Controller and Interaction intake.** GIVEN a Controller double whose `moved` state toggles each tick and a registry fixture `NOISE_PER_TICK` of (walk 1, run 4, carry 1), WHEN six ticks pass, THEN `pending` is 6,000 mAU (walk) or 24,000 mAU (run); changing the fixture to run 5 changes the result to 30,000 with no recompile (no literal in Attention); a push landing after the hook in a tick integrates on the next run; a DOWNED seat pushes nothing; one runner pushes 0 to 24 AU per run. [AT-3, E-21, F-1, PCI:560]

### C. The level, the baseline, the threshold

- **AC-AT-11 (auto) Exact return to baseline.** GIVEN any `L` in `[B, L_CEIL]` and zero input, WHEN runs execute, THEN `L` reaches `B` exactly (never stalls above it), for `L - B` of 1, 5 and 5,700,000 mAU and for `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 300, 1800 and 3600. [AT-5, F-5, LSG:464]
- **AC-AT-12 (auto) No overflow.** GIVEN the fixture of 96 emitters at output 1000 and reach 384, every weight at its cap (4000), six seats pushing `LOUD` in one run and a `power_use` of 10,000 W, WHEN a run executes, THEN each F-1, F-3, F-4 and F-5 intermediate equals the oracle's arbitrary-precision value and is below 2^62, `pending_mAU[src]` equals `L_CEIL` and `L'` equals `L_CEIL`. (A 64-bit wrap cannot be observed in the sim, so the oracle is the witness.) [AT-28, F-1, F-5, E-4, E-7]
- **AC-AT-13 (auto) The ceiling.** GIVEN any input sequence, WHEN runs execute, THEN `B <= L <= L_CEIL` after every run. [AT-5, F-5]
- **AC-AT-14 (auto) The worked integrator example.** GIVEN `B` 300,000, `L` 2,000,000 and the run input of F-5's example (30,324 mAU), WHEN one run executes, THEN `dec` is 5,667 and `L'` is 2,024,657. [F-5]
- **AC-AT-57a (auto) Calibration trace: home idle.** GIVEN a harness driving `push_stimulus` and stubbed `emitters()` and `power_use` (no scene tree) with the home-ship fixture, WHEN 10 minutes elapse, THEN TELL is never entered and `L` settles at 1,679 AU (+/- 1 AU of the integer model, +/- 3 percent of the closed form). [AT-5, F-5, F-25]
- **AC-AT-57b (auto) Walking with the beam.** GIVEN a derelict with the home ship lit, WHEN the player walks with the beam, THEN `L` settles at 3,997 AU (`level_pm` 648) and TELL is never entered. [F-25]
- **AC-AT-57c (auto) Running with the beam from baseline.** GIVEN running with the beam from `B`, THEN `L` crosses `theta` in 2,412 ticks (+/- 3 percent of the closed form). [F-25]
- **AC-AT-57d (auto) Running from the home-idle level.** GIVEN running with the beam from the home-idle level, THEN it crosses in 1,476 ticks (+/- 3 percent). [F-25]
- **AC-AT-57e (auto) A short job.** GIVEN a 6 s tool job followed by 30 s of stillness, THEN TELL is never entered. [F-25]
- **AC-AT-57f (auto) The job chain.** GIVEN the chain fixture in `intake` test data (6 s tool jobs with the beam on, 5 s gaps, one `LOUD` at each job end, home ship lit at 766 mAU per tick), THEN TELL is entered between 30 s and 90 s. [F-25]
- **AC-AT-57g (auto) Model agreement.** GIVEN each trace above, THEN the integer model agrees with `E(t) = E_ss - (E_ss - E_0) x exp(-t / tau)` within 3 percent. [F-25]
- **AC-AT-15 (data-lint) Baseline monotone.** GIVEN `B_table`, WHEN loaded, THEN it is non-decreasing with ring and `theta > B` at every row (so `level_pm` never divides by zero). Negative fixture: a ring-2 row of 250 AU. [AT-6, F-7, F-8, E-10]
- **AC-AT-16 (auto traces + data-lint ring rows) Abortability and the dark floor.** GIVEN each `B_table` row and the dark fixture, WHEN the integer model runs F-5 from `L_CEIL` at `r_dark`, THEN `x_dark <= theta_HOLD - ATT_MARGIN` and the level is below `theta_HOLD - ATT_MARGIN` within `(TELL_MIN_TICKS - 6) / 6` = 99 runs: 90, 93 and 95 runs at rings 1, 2 and 3 with `N_GLOW_DARK` 0, and 93, 96 and 98 runs with the two wake glows; a scripted player double that zeroes every input at entry level `L_CEIL` aborts for every `tell_len` in `[TELL_MIN_TICKS, TELL_MAX_TICKS]` at every passing ring. The shipped rows are evaluated with `N_GLOW_DARK` 2 (the two wake glows, DA-16), so boot passes at 93, 96 and 98 runs. **Negative fixture:** a ring-3 row of 600 AU with the two wake glows needs 100 runs and the lint returns the named error `ATT_DARK_FLOOR_RING3`, refusing that data set. [AT-6, AT-10, F-15, F-16]
- **AC-AT-64 (data-lint) `abortability_play`.** GIVEN each `B_table` row, WHEN loaded, THEN `t_decay(ring) + REACT_ALLOW_TICKS <= TELL_DRAW_LO - 6` (720, 738 and 750 against 894 at the placeholders; 738, 756 and 768 with the two wake glows), and a scripted player double that zeroes all inputs 180 ticks after beat 0 aborts every tell drawn in `[TELL_DRAW_LO, TELL_DRAW_HI]`. Negative fixture: `TELL_DRAW_LO` 750 at ring 3 (fails). [AT-8, F-15b]
- **AC-AT-17 (auto) The four-guard entry.** GIVEN `L' >= theta`, WHEN exactly one of {phase not CALM, `T < refract_until_T`, `T < rearm_until_T`, `L' < theta`} is true, THEN no TELL starts; WHEN none is true (including `T == refract_until_T`), THEN TELL starts. [AT-7, F-9, E-14, E-15]
- **AC-AT-85 (auto) Baseline and ring.** GIVEN seats in ring-1 and ring-2 sections, WHEN `B` is computed, THEN it is `B_table[2]`; WHEN the ring-2 seat dies or leaves, THEN `B` falls and `L` decays toward the new floor with no reset; WHEN `B` rises above `L`, THEN `L` becomes `B` in the same run, `level_pm` is 0 and no TELL starts; a ring unresolved by Web Map or the record hint falls to ring 1 and logs once per process. [AT-6, F-8, E-8, E-9, E-58, E-61]
- **AC-AT-119 (auto, Vertical Slice; deferred: blocked on Depth Rings & Scarcity) Depth is a measurable lever.** GIVEN the per-ring light and power stimulus gain data and one recorded behaviour trace (identical per-tick inputs), WHEN F-5 is integrated from each ring's baseline with the trace replayed at ring 1 and at ring 3, THEN the ring-3 level reaches `theta` in measurably fewer ticks than at ring 1, by at least `DEPTH_LEVER_MIN_PM` of the ring-1 crossing time (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 100 permille; Depth Rings & Scarcity owns and registers it), and `I_noise` is identical at both rings tick by tick (the gain is never applied to noise). If Depth Rings cannot satisfy this it surfaces a CONFLICT, it does not silently weaken the lever. [AT-6, P3, P5]
- **AC-AT-86 (auto) The published level.** GIVEN `L` 3,000,000 and `B` 300,000, WHEN `level_pm` is computed, THEN it is 473; at `L` = `B` it is 0; at `L >= theta` it is 1000. [F-7, E-10]
- **AC-AT-104 (auto) Empty world.** GIVEN no loaded section, WHEN runs execute, THEN `I_light` and `I_power` are 0, `L` reaches `B` exactly and no TELL starts. [E-6]
- **AC-AT-105 (auto) Pinned Moment A trace.** GIVEN ring 1, `N_GLOW_DARK` 0 and a player who enters at `theta` and zeroes every input, WHEN runs execute, THEN `L` leaves `theta_HOLD` after 80 runs and the abort completes 594 ticks after the stop; the test asserts those numbers at the placeholder data and records them against `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` (600), so a retune that moves the 6-tick resume margin shows as a visible diff. The 594 is 480 ticks to cross `theta_HOLD` plus 114 ticks of hold (the first run under the line counts as run 1, so the hold adds 19 runs); an abort therefore usually forfeits the partial, the deliberate price of restraint (the CR-TIMELINE stance, F-15b). [F-15b, E-26]
- **AC-AT-106 (data-lint) Knob ranges and cliffs.** GIVEN each knob in Tuning Knobs, WHEN set one step outside its safe range, THEN boot refuses with a named reason (the Tuning Knobs row is the authority: `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1200 to 3600 and below 1817 at the ring-3 test row with the two wake glows; F-5's 300 is the formula's arithmetic domain, not the knob range); and each of the six cliff values (`TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1817, `HOLD_PM` 797, `L_CEIL_PM` 1023, `ATT_MARGIN` 63, `W_P` 117, a ring-3 baseline of 595 AU), applied alone at the ring-3 test row with the two wake glows, fails the F-15 lint while the value one step inside passes. [Tuning Knobs, F-15, F-16]

### D. The tell

- **AC-AT-18 (auto) A restore does not change the length.** GIVEN a snapshot taken mid-TELL, WHEN restored and continued, THEN `tell_len`, `breach_due_T`, `rng_counter`, `tell_beat` and `beat_next_off` equal those of uninterrupted play. [AT-8, E-45]
- **AC-AT-19 (auto) The arming seam.** GIVEN `attention_arm_tell`, WHEN called in CALM with `tell_len` 1800, THEN TELL enters at the next run with beat 0 published in that run and `rng_counter` unchanged; with 100 it is clamped to 600; outside CALM it is rejected `NOT_CALM`; a PLAYER-origin submission is rejected with the kernel's origin reason. [AT-21, F-10, E-19]
- **AC-AT-99 (auto + review) The arming seam ignores the timers.** GIVEN CALM with `rearm_until_T` and `refract_until_T` in the future, WHEN `attention_arm_tell {900}` is submitted by SYSTEM, THEN TELL enters at the next run (the CALM requirement still binds); review: the command is listed in the control manifest as non-gameplay. [AT-21, F-9, E-19]
- **AC-AT-20 (auto) The two-channel floor, headless half.** GIVEN a jitter provider injected into the schedule (dependency injection per the coding standard; production uses the integer hash) forced to all +`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS`, all -`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` and all 0, plus the real hash over `tell_epoch` 1 to 10,000, for every `tell_len` in {600, 606, ..., 1800} and a run-phase offset of 0 to 5 ticks, WHEN the schedule runs un-aborted, THEN at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` beats are published in `[tell_start_T, tell_start_T + TELL_MIN_TICKS)`. The recorded minimum (5 at the placeholders: offsets 0, 158, 307, 448, 580) is informational: a retune that lowers it to 4 still passes and one that lowers it to 3 fails. The test needs the injection seam (flagged to the lead-programmer). [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-21 (auto) Beat spacing.** GIVEN any epoch, WHEN beats publish, THEN nominal offsets are non-decreasing by at least `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS`; with every jitter forced to -`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` the smallest gap is `max(BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS, BEAT_END_TICKS - BEAT_JITTER_TICKS)`; no beat is suppressed in the final gap before `breach_due_T` (a beat whose nominal offset is below `tell_len` but within `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS` of it is still published); and an un-aborted 1,800-tick tell publishes between 19 and 22 beats (closed form 20.6; the band to be confirmed against the integer model by the tester, see Gaps). [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-22 (auto) G4 save-equivalence.** GIVEN snapshots at the tick before TELL entry, mid-TELL, at the due run (pre and post), mid-BREACH, mid-SUBSIDING and in the refractory, WHEN each is restored and run forward, THEN `state_hash` equals uninterrupted play at every tick and no cue fires on the restore itself. [AT-1, AT-27, AT-28, E-45 to E-47]
- **AC-AT-23 (auto) No reroll.** GIVEN a restore mid-TELL, WHEN the tell completes, THEN `rng_counter` advanced exactly once for that epoch and the next organic tell's length equals the uninterrupted run's. [AT-1, AT-8]
- **AC-AT-24 (auto) Audio-first by construction.** GIVEN a TELL entry, WHEN the entry run completes, THEN `tell_beat` 0 and `phase` TELL are published by the same run, and no filament-resolve event for that epoch is emitted earlier. [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-82 (auto) Two crossings, one tell.** GIVEN two `LOUD` pushes in one run that crosses `theta` and a `LOUD` in the entry run, WHEN the run executes, THEN `tell_epoch` increases by exactly 1, `rng_counter` by exactly 1, `tell_beat` 0 is published in that run, `tell_len` is in `[TELL_DRAW_LO, TELL_DRAW_HI]` and no abort check runs on the entry run. [E-12, E-13, F-10]
- **AC-AT-25 (auto) The abort path.** GIVEN a TELL and `L` held under `theta_HOLD` for `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` (20 runs, the first run under counting as 1), WHEN the 20th run executes, THEN `phase` is CALM, `tell_outcome` is ABORTED, no STRESS is deposited, no `set_beacon` is submitted, `tell_beat` is frozen, `L` is unchanged and `rearm_until_T = T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`. [AT-10, F-14]
- **AC-AT-83 (auto) A dip resets the abort counter.** GIVEN a TELL, WHEN `L` is under `theta_HOLD` for 19 consecutive runs, at or above it for 1, then under it for 19 again, THEN no early abort occurs, the at-due decision still applies, and `below_hold_runs` reads 0 after the recovery run. [E-17, F-14]
- **AC-AT-26 (auto) The abort at the due run.** GIVEN `T >= breach_due_T` and `L' < theta_HOLD` (including the case where the early-abort counter also reaches its limit on that run), WHEN the run executes, THEN the result is one transition to CALM with ABORTED, never BREACH; at `L' == theta_HOLD` the breach commits. [AT-10, E-11, E-16]
- **AC-AT-84 (auto + data-lint) Abort relief.** GIVEN `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` 1000, WHEN a tell aborts, THEN `L` is unchanged (AC-AT-25); GIVEN 700, THEN `L' = min(L, B + 700 x (theta - B) div 1000)`; 600 is the lowest accepted value and 599 refuses boot. [AT-10, F-14, Tuning Knobs]
- **AC-AT-27 (auto) Rearm.** GIVEN an abort at `T`, WHEN `L` returns above `theta` before `T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`, THEN no TELL starts until the first run at or after that tick, and the TELL then has `tell_elapsed` 0. [AT-10, E-14]
- **AC-AT-52 (data-lint) Rearm and refractory against the retry.** GIVEN the tell and breach data, WHEN loaded, THEN `TELL_REARM_TICKS >= AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` and `REFRACT_TICKS >= AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`. Negative fixture: `TELL_REARM_TICKS` 200. [AT-10, AT-29, F-21]
- **AC-AT-38 (auto) `tell_remaining`.** GIVEN any epoch, WHEN sampled every run, THEN it lies in `[0, TELL_MAX_TICKS]`, is 0 outside TELL and is non-increasing within an epoch. [AT-16, F-11]
- **AC-AT-69 (auto) Tier.** GIVEN `tell_elapsed` 594 and 600, WHEN runs execute, THEN `tier` is 1 and 2; outside TELL it is 0; the whole floor window is tier 1. (Tier 3 from 1200 is AC-AT-81.) [AT-9, F-13]
- **AC-AT-91 (auto) Published fields by phase.** GIVEN a replay passing through CALM, TELL, BREACH, SUBSIDING and an abort, WHEN `attention.view` is sampled every run, THEN every field in the States and Transitions table (`tell_active`, `breach_phase` reporting TELL as CALM, `tell_remaining`, `tell_elapsed`, `tier`, `tell_beat`, `exogenous_permitted`, the commanded beacon, `tell_outcome` NONE / ABORTED / BREACHED with its persistence rules, `tell_epoch` +1 per entry) equals the table's row for that phase. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-116 (auto) The act-to-tell chain.** GIVEN a `LOUD` push that takes `L` across `theta` in tick T with all four guards open, WHEN the sim runs, THEN TELL is entered at the first run at or after T (at most 6 ticks later, or 12 if the push landed after the hook in its tick) with `tell_beat` 0 published in that same run. [AT-3, AT-9, Game Feel]
- **AC-AT-47 (manual, Visual/Feel; deferred: audio half blocked on Adaptive Audio & Tells; visual half provisional on the Presenter) Each sense is sufficient alone.** GIVEN a 600-tick armed tell with the beam on in a lit region, WHEN captured once with the audio muted and once audio-only (no video), THEN the muted capture shows at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` filament-resolve events inside the cone in the first 600 ticks and the audio-only capture has at least that many audible beats. The headless half is AC-AT-20. Both captures retained in `production/qa/evidence/attention/` with the art-director's sign-off. [AT-9, AT-24, ART:954]

### E. The breach

- **AC-AT-28 (auto) Breach entry.** GIVEN a TELL due run with `L' >= theta_HOLD` and a locus body of S sections, WHEN the run executes, THEN `phase` is BREACH, `breach_enter_T = T`, M17 `deposit_trace(STRESS, s, magnitude = BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT)` is called for each section in ascending `section_id`, at most `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` per run, the whole body is covered within `ceil(S / STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN)` runs, and the magnitude argument is the registry constant (not a literal). [AT-11, F-18]
- **AC-AT-29 (auto) The beacon command.** GIVEN BREACH entry, WHEN the next tick applies, THEN `set_beacon(on)` is submitted as a SYSTEM command at T+1 with `max_age_ticks` 120 (for a derelict locus too: the one home-ship beacon); at SUBSIDING entry `set_beacon(off)` is submitted. GIVEN each rejection reason `NOT_LIVE`, `NO_SUCH_EMITTER` and `NOT_BEACON`, THEN the machine still advances, the failure is logged once and the command is retried once per run for at most `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS`. [AT-11, AT-13, AT-18, E-30]
- **AC-AT-58 (auto) Breach teeth, seeded fault.** GIVEN a golden file produced by running the Graph alone, with and without STRESS, on a locus body seeded with one active overload (a cable) and one fatigued plate, WHEN the BREACH runs its full `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, THEN the `hp` loss on each seeded subject with STRESS equals the golden STRESS value exactly and the ratio to the no-STRESS value is `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT / 1000` (2.0) within an integer tolerance of one hp unit per subject per armed step (the Graph owner confirms the tolerance). The clean-ship companion stays labelled contract-only (it tests the tell, not the teeth) and is not a model of an MVP derelict: the content requirement is AC-AT-117. [AT-11, E-35]
- **AC-AT-117 (data-lint, authoring; deferred: blocked on Derelict Authoring's data for derelicts A and B) Every possible locus body has teeth.** GIVEN the MVP derelict A and derelict B data and every body that can be a locus (each derelict alone and each welded to the home-ship), WHEN the authoring lint runs, THEN each such body contains at least one Graph subject authored active with an overload (a cable) or a fatigue fault (a plate), the same state the AC-AT-58 seeded scenario uses; a body with none fails with the named check `DERELICT_LOCUS_BODY_NO_FAULT` (Derelict Authoring's name, adopted), whose severity and stage are Derelict Authoring's (WARN at CI, ERROR once OQ-DA-30 is answered); Attention requests no seventh BOOT rule beyond DA-41's six. The pass condition for derelict A is open (OQ-AT-24). The home-ship body is exempt (its faults are the crew's to repair). This turns AC-AT-58's seeded fault into a content requirement. [AT-11, E-35, AC-AT-58]
- **AC-AT-31 (auto) STRESS never lapses mid-BREACH.** GIVEN a full-length BREACH, WHEN the Graph's STRESS armed state is sampled every run (through the Graph's named read, or a test-only accessor until the Graph owner names one; see Gaps), THEN every section of the body is armed at every run after its first deposit, and the coverage margin `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS - STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS - STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS` is positive. [AT-12, F-18]
- **AC-AT-32 (auto) Every end clause.** GIVEN BREACH, WHEN each of the following is driven in turn, THEN the machine moves to SUBSIDING: `D >= BREACH_MAX_TICKS` (3,600); `D >= BREACH_MIN_TICKS` and 50 consecutive runs under `theta_HOLD` (earliest at D = 1,194); the last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry leaving a non-home `locus_body`; an empty non-home locus body at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` (evaluated at entry only). GIVEN both end clauses true on one run, THEN there is one transition and the deposits stop. [AT-12, F-17, E-18, E-33]
- **AC-AT-33 (data-lint) The refresh fits inside the hold.** GIVEN the breach data and the cited `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS`, WHEN loaded, THEN `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS + STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS < STRESS_HOLD_STEPS` and `STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS x STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN >= S_max` (24 >= 14). Negative fixture: `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS` 598. [AT-12, F-18]
- **AC-AT-93 (auto) The burst schedule.** GIVEN a 5-section body and `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, WHEN the BREACH runs, THEN deposits occur at run q = 0, then q = 300, 301 and 302, then at q = 600 only if the breach is still running, with no deposit on the run that ends it. GIVEN a fixture with `STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS` 1, `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 8 and a body of 9 sections (above the burst capacity of 8), THEN the second F-18 lint fails at authoring and, if the data is forced through, the cursor wraps and the remaining section waits for the next burst. [AT-12, F-18, E-37]
- **AC-AT-34 (auto) The level keeps integrating.** GIVEN BREACH with the player running, WHEN runs execute, THEN `L` keeps rising, the breach does not end before `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, and a quiet player ends it at the earliest at D = 1,194. [AT-12]
- **AC-AT-35 (auto) The subside reset (entry).** GIVEN SUBSIDING entry with `L` above the residual, WHEN it executes, THEN `L' = min(L, B + POST_PM x (theta - B) div 1000)` (2,580,000 at `B` 300,000), `set_beacon(off)` is submitted and no further STRESS is deposited; GIVEN `L` below the residual at entry, THEN `L` is unchanged. [AT-13, F-19]
- **AC-AT-98 (auto) SUBSIDING and its end.** GIVEN SUBSIDING entry at T, WHEN `T + SUBSIDE_TICKS` is reached, THEN the first run at or after it returns to CALM, sets `tell_outcome` BREACHED and `refract_until_T = T_end + REFRACT_TICKS`; `exogenous_permitted` is true until that run. [AT-13, F-19]
- **AC-AT-36 (auto) The refractory.** GIVEN the end of SUBSIDING, WHEN `L >= theta` during `REFRACT_TICKS`, THEN no TELL starts before `refract_until_T`, the level keeps integrating, and the first legal run past it enters TELL. [AT-13, E-14]
- **AC-AT-60 (auto) The transition table.** GIVEN the state table, WHEN every ordered pair of states is attempted, THEN only CALM to TELL, TELL to CALM (two guards), TELL to BREACH, BREACH to SUBSIDING and SUBSIDING to CALM occur, CALM to BREACH, TELL to SUBSIDING and BREACH to TELL never do, and `phase` changes only inside `attention.step`. [AT-13, States and Transitions]
- **AC-AT-37 (auto) The locus.** GIVEN ledgers {A 3,000,000; B 3,000,000; home 1,400,000} at TELL entry, WHEN the locus is chosen, THEN it is the lower `section_id` of A and B; given the locus section cut away mid-event, THEN it falls back to the home-ship's lowest `section_id`, `locus_body` is recomputed and `tell_remaining` is unchanged. [AT-14, F-6, E-27, E-28]
- **AC-AT-87 (auto) The ledger and the fixed locus.** GIVEN `ledger[A]` 3,000,000 and `I_s` 30,324, WHEN a run executes, THEN `ledger'[A]` is 3,020,324 and the array stays sorted by `section_id`; a ledger that decays to 0 persists as 0; once a TELL has fixed the locus, a later change of ledger order does not move it before the epoch ends. [AT-14, F-6, E-28]
- **AC-AT-59 (auto) The decoy knob.** GIVEN `LOCUS_OCC_PREF_PM` 1000, WHEN a lit unoccupied derelict has the higher ledger, THEN the locus is that derelict exactly as AT-14 states; with a lower value the occupied section can win. [AT-14, F-6]
- **AC-AT-46 (auto, provisional; deferred: blocked on Web Map & Transit for the notification name) Pod-hop clauses.** GIVEN a non-home `locus_body` whose last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry (`locus_occupants_at_entry`) leaves it during BREACH, WHEN the next run executes, THEN BREACH ends; GIVEN a living player who was never inside a non-home locus body (Moment C), THEN BREACH does not end by clause (iii) and ends at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` by X10; GIVEN two occupants at entry and one leaves, THEN BREACH continues; a pod hop during TELL does not interrupt it; a departure from a home-ship locus does not end BREACH. The derivable-now half runs against Ship Body's `actor_section`. [AT-22, E-23]
- **AC-AT-42 (auto) The beacon within a run.** GIVEN BREACH entry with a live home-ship beacon, WHEN two ticks elapse, THEN the beacon emitter is `lit` and the emitter list shows it; the world holds exactly one BEACON (`BEACON_COUNT`). [AT-18, DA-24]
- **AC-AT-65 (auto) Post-restore beacon reconciliation.** GIVEN a snapshot taken in BREACH with the SYSTEM command dropped on reload (beacon off, `beacon_commanded` on), WHEN restored, THEN `post_restore` changes no `phase` and submits no command, and the first `attention.step` run submits `set_beacon(on)` exactly once; with the states already matching, no command is submitted. [AT-27, E-48]
- **AC-AT-92 (auto) A dropped STRESS deposit.** GIVEN a Horror double that saturates `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` before Attention's P3 call, WHEN a BREACH enters, THEN the dropped sections are unstressed, `TellAudit` records no violation and the next burst re-arms them; with the double's budget at `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP - STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` all eight deposits are honoured. [AT-11, E-29, C22]
- **AC-AT-94 (auto) The STRESS tail.** GIVEN a breach that ends at D = 1,194 and one that ends at 3,600, WHEN SUBSIDING ends, THEN the Graph's STRESS is still armed for 1,206 ticks and for 612 ticks respectively, Attention made no deposit after the ending run, `phase` reads CALM, and Attention's published surfaces contain no all-clear mapping. [E-34, F-18]
- **AC-AT-94b (integration, auto) Death restore.** GIVEN every seat DEAD mid-TELL and the Save/Load death stub restoring the newest verified autosave, WHEN the same inputs replay, THEN `rng_counter`, `tell_len`, `breach_due_T` and the beat sequence are identical to the first run and no onset cue fires on the restore. This is the MVP death handling the user chose. [E-22, AT-27]

### F. View, truth channel, presentation

- **AC-AT-39 (source-lint) Reader scope.** GIVEN the readers of `attention.view`, WHEN scanned, THEN no PRESENTATION subscriber reads `tell_remaining`, `tell_progress_pm` does not exist, and `tell_remaining` is read only by Save/Load, SIM readers and the DIAGNOSTIC Debug Overlay. Negative fixture: a presenter stub that reads `tell_remaining`. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-67 (source-lint) `phase`, not `breach_phase`.** GIVEN the Adaptive Audio subscriber (when written) and any double, WHEN scanned, THEN it reads `phase` and never `breach_phase`; and `breach_phase` reports TELL as CALM. [AT-16, C5]
- **AC-AT-40 (auto + source-lint) No `PERCEPTION_OUT`.** GIVEN the registry entry for each `attention.view` field and for the view builder, WHEN its source blocks are listed, THEN every source block has `class SIM` and none is `PERCEPTION_OUT`; the builder's source contains no read of a `PERCEPTION_OUT` block id; and the field set equals exactly the AT-16 table (adding a field fails the test). [AT-16, AT-17, AC-SL-95a, AC-SL-121]
- **AC-AT-41 (source-lint) The truth channel.** GIVEN the Perception Filter's write set and Sanity's, WHEN scanned, THEN neither contains an Attention cue (a filament, a bed beat, a beacon state), the Filter's anchor list includes the tell cues, and Attention reads no sanity value. [AT-17, E-55]
- **AC-AT-66 (source-lint + review) No meter anywhere.** GIVEN the UI and presentation source roots (HUD, Chart Table and menus, In-World Instruments, Settings, Onboarding) and `assets/`, with the allow-list `{Debug Overlay root, diagnostics channel, tests/}` in the lint config, WHEN scanned for the tokens `level_pm`, `level_mAU`, `tell_remaining` and `attention_level`, THEN there are no hits outside the allow-list; the AT-16 named readers outside those roots (the Presenter and Audio for quantised `level_pm`; Save/Load and Horror for `tell_remaining`) and Attention's own module (`level_mAU`) are out of the scan's scope by construction, mirroring Debug Overlay AC-DO-17 (OQ-DO-22); and GIVEN the review checklist (the accessibility settings screen and each HUD spec have no field that displays or sonifies the level; no caption, vignette or glyph), THEN every box is ticked; the Debug Overlay is absent from release builds; and GIVEN the release export's file list, THEN no path under `overlay_source_root` and no name in `dev_artifact_list` appears (registry `overlay_build_gate`). [UI Requirements, AT-26, ART:741, :954]
- **AC-AT-103 (source-lint) No captions.** GIVEN the project, WHEN scanned, THEN no caption field or caption string exists for tell beats, and `attention.view` carries no field named or typed as displayed text. [AT-24, ART:741]
- **AC-AT-43 (review + source-lint) The suppressant contract.** GIVEN the Suit and Attention contract text, WHEN reviewed, THEN `tool_charge(player_id, tool_suppressant)`, `consume_charge(player_id, def_id) -> ok` and `OUT_OF_CHARGES` are the only names, no effect verb ships at MVP, and the wording is "repel or distract". [AT-19]
- **AC-AT-44 (manual, Visual/Feel, screenshots at every zoom rung) Filaments only in lit regions of eligible emitters.** GIVEN a tell, WHEN captured at each integer zoom rung `k` in `[1, k_auto + 2]` in five scenes (beam on; beam off with a FIXTURE lit; beam off with a NODE_LANTERN or DEPLOYABLE lit; only a BEACON, `fx_cryo_lamp` or a FAULT_GLOW lit; nothing lit), THEN filaments appear only inside the lit region of a BEAM, FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN or DEPLOYABLE and never in unlit space; none is drawn in the excluded-emitters-only scene or the nothing-lit scene; filaments are 1 art px wide without anti-aliasing smear at the tips, and a resolved strand still reads at the zoom-out rung (trunk at least 2 px, the 50 percent scale test of ART 5.6). Captures named `AC-AT-44-k[rung]-[scene]-[date].png`, art-director sign-off. If the producer takes the cone-only scope fallback, the fixture and lantern scenes are expected to show none and the record says so. [AT-20, ART:302, AV-13, AV-14]
- **AC-AT-45 (data-lint + manual) The palette.** GIVEN the presenter's data and code, WHEN scanned, THEN every colour literal is a member of the art bible's iridescence list at ART:1371 (copied into the test data with its source line); GIVEN the retained engine screenshots of AC-AT-44 and AC-AT-111 under Tungsten, Chemlight and the beacon sweep, WHEN every strand pixel is sampled, THEN none falls in the violet arc (hue 270 to 330) or the flesh range (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 percent, V 45 to 85 percent), the band peak is at or under `BAND_PEAK_LSTAR_MAX` and no bloom appears at the glow threshold. - **AC-AT-53 (auto) The camera does nothing.** GIVEN an identical input trace, WHEN played through CALM, TELL and BREACH, THEN the camera transform and zoom, the beam's `output_pm` and `reach_px`, the global darkness level and the input map are byte-identical across the three; the only lighting changes allowed are Lighting's own tier-driven lamp gutter on FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN and the BEACON emitter, each checked against Lighting's criteria. [AT-20, PIPE:151, AV-1]
- **AC-AT-49a (auto) Quantised cues.** GIVEN `level_pm` swept from 0 to 1000 and back with 20-permille jitter around each edge, WHEN the cue step is computed, THEN it takes only the values 0 to `CUE_STEPS`, rises at the edges 550, 663, 775 and 888, falls only at 525, 638, 750 and 863, produces no cue change faster than one per edge crossing, and pre-tell cue events are absent from `TellAudit` and from the floor-window beat count. - **AC-AT-49b (data-lint) The cue-floor calibration.** GIVEN the presentation data, WHEN loaded, THEN `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM >= max over rings of walk_beam_pm + CUE_MARGIN_PM` (406, 417 and 425 plus 100 give 525, so 550 passes and the superseded 350 fails). Negative fixture: 350. [AT-26, F-24, C23]
- **AC-AT-49c (auto) No continuous mapping.** GIVEN `level_pm` swept across the interior of one cue step, WHEN the presenter's output state is compared, THEN it is byte-identical (no density, size, brightness, band intensity or dust amplitude changes), because the token scan of AC-AT-66 cannot see an indirect mapping. [AT-26, AV-6]
- **AC-AT-50 (auto) Cues fire on transitions only.** GIVEN a save made in CALM with ABORTED, in TELL, in BREACH and in SUBSIDING, WHEN each is loaded, THEN no onset, breach-enter, release or subside cue fires on the load. [AT-27, E-49]
- **AC-AT-102 (manual, Visual/Feel) Accessibility and the beam.** GIVEN the reduce-motion and reduce-flashing settings, WHEN a tell is captured, THEN filament motion becomes a density change (the per-beat density step still rises), the beacon becomes the static lit lens, the dust turns in the beam and no hull-stress hairline is drawn; retained as `AC-AT-102-[setting]-[date].png` in `production/qa/evidence/attention/` with art-director sign-off. [AT-20, AT-24, AV-14, Accessibility and reduced-effect behaviour]
- **AC-AT-107 (auto, presenter double) Guaranteed resolve.** GIVEN a lit region with at least one unresolved eligible site, WHEN each beat publishes, THEN at least one strand resolves in that cone; a cone with no eligible site on screen is the only exception and the harness logs it; and the visual resolve count per lit region in the first `TELL_MIN_TICKS` equals the published beat count (the visual half of AC-AT-20). [AV-7]
- **AC-AT-118 (auto, presenter double) Strand eligibility by emitter kind.** GIVEN a presenter double and one emitter of each kind (`BEAM`, `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN`, `DEPLOYABLE`, `BEACON`, `FAULT_GLOW`) plus the `fx_cryo_lamp` EMERGENCY fixture, WHEN site eligibility is evaluated at a site in each lit region and in unlit space, THEN it is true for the first four, false for `BEACON`, `FAULT_GLOW` and `fx_cryo_lamp`, and false in unlit space; a site lit by both an eligible and an excluded emitter is eligible through the eligible one only. [AT-20, AV-7, AV-14, LO-20]
- **AC-AT-108 (manual, Visual/Feel) Ring separation.** GIVEN a ring-3 CALM frame sequence and a ring-3 TELL tier-1 sequence with brightness equalised, WHEN shown to a tester, THEN they are told apart by shape and motion alone; retained captures with art-director sign-off. [AV-16]
- **AC-AT-109 (manual, Visual/Feel; deferred: blocked on Sanity & Hallucination Rules for the fringe asset) Rim contention.** GIVEN a strand and a stage-2 violet fringe on the same rim, WHEN compared hue-removed, in a single still frame and motion-frozen, THEN each comparison is classified correctly above chance, and a second client shows the strands and not the fringe. A fringe that cannot be tangential, continuous, breathing and per-viewer is a CONFLICT for the creative-director. [AV-17, cone-rim contention]
- **AC-AT-110 (source-lint + auto) Not an emitter.** GIVEN the presenter, WHEN scanned, THEN no presenter output is registered as an emitter or appears in `emitters()`, and no Attention input reads a presenter value; GIVEN a replay with the presenter on and off, THEN `I_light` is identical. [AV-15]
- **AC-AT-111 (manual, Visual/Feel) Release is not a beat.** GIVEN captures of an abort and of SUBSIDING, THEN the retractions contain no band-slide, BREACH entry shows the snap and then no motion at the rim, and no success or all-clear visual appears; retained with art-director sign-off. [AV-9, AV-10, AV-11]
- **AC-AT-112 (auto + manual) Restore, pause, hitch (visual).** GIVEN the first rendered frame after a restore in each phase, THEN the correct end state shows with no transition animation; GIVEN a stall of several beats, THEN at most one beat plays and none is replayed. [AV-12]
- **AC-AT-113 (source-lint) Exclusive assets.** GIVEN the project, WHEN scanned, THEN no consumer other than the Attention Presenter references `web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*` or the band-slide animation, and no Sanity, Horror or Perception Filter asset tag shares the family. [AV-18]
- **AC-AT-114 (manual, Visual/Feel) Readability.** GIVEN a resolved strand crossing a failing pipe or a trail, THEN it never out-contrasts it (the ART 3.5 trail-contrast test, once its number exists) and `STRAND_VIEW_CAP` is never exceeded. [AV-8]
- **AC-AT-115 (auto) The dedupe pair.** GIVEN a replay with an aborted tell followed by a second tell, WHEN `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` is sampled each run, THEN the pair is strictly increasing lexicographically across the whole replay (the new epoch's beat 0 follows the old epoch's last beat); a `tell_beat` jump of N > 1 between polls is one observable change. The audio behaviour that consumes it is AC-AT-78. [AT-16, AUD-3]

### G. Scope, edges and determinism

- **AC-AT-48 (source-lint) One-way edges.** GIVEN the sources of the Graph, Controller, Suit, Interaction verb data, camera, Failure & Trail and Item DB, WHEN scanned, THEN none reads an Attention symbol; and Attention's source reads nothing of Lighting, Audio, Sanity, Horror, the Perception Filter, HUD, Instruments, Failure & Trail, Camera or Save/Load. Negative fixture: a Graph stub that reads `level_pm`. [AT-25]
- **AC-AT-55 (auto) Seat state.** GIVEN seats in states IDLE, DOWNED, DEAD and IN_POD, WHEN `B` and the pod hold are computed, THEN DOWNED counts as living, DEAD does not, and IN_POD holds the departure ring; with every seat DEAD `B` holds its last value. [AT-23, AT-31, E-21, E-63]
- **AC-AT-56 (auto) Diagnostics are inert.** GIVEN a replay with the diagnostic subscriber on and off, WHEN `state_hash` is compared at every tick, THEN it is identical and no sim path reads a diagnostic counter. [AT-32]
- **AC-AT-100 (auto) Diagnostics content.** GIVEN a replay with several tells, aborts and breaches, WHEN the diagnostic subscriber is read, THEN it holds the counts of entries, aborts and breaches, ticks per phase, the per-channel contribution before each TELL entry, abort latency and locus occupancy at breach, and AC-AT-80's abort share can be computed from it. [AT-32]
- **AC-AT-51 (data-lint) The event-defer invariant.** GIVEN the cited and configured values, WHEN loaded, THEN `TELL_MAX_TICKS + BREACH_MAX_TICKS + SUBSIDE_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS < AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (6,900 < 7,200), re-checked across `gate_tell_mode` FAIR and STRICT; negative fixture: `BREACH_MAX_TICKS` 3,900 fails. [AT-29, F-20]
- **AC-AT-91b (integration, auto) Save/Load gate.** GIVEN the real autosave gate, WHEN an autosave comes due during TELL, THEN the FAIR-mode window stays live because `breach_phase` is CALM, under both `gate_tell_mode` FAIR and STRICT; a save taken mid-TELL restores the full tell. [AT-16, E-51]
- **AC-AT-61 (review) MVP scope.** GIVEN the GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN multiple tiers, per-ring thresholds, the suppressant verb, per-player attribution, rate-coupled length and co-op scaling appear only as named Vertical Slice addenda, the per-ring stimulus gain appears only as the pillar-flagged Vertical Slice requirement of AT-6, no rule absorbs any of them, and phantom tells and false calm appear nowhere as an addendum (rejected by pillar, AT-17). [AT-30]
- **AC-AT-70 (auto) Restore failures are hard.** GIVEN a block with a `schema_version` outside `supported_versions [1]`, an `L` above `L_CEIL`, an incoherent phase, an unknown section id, or an `rng_counter` greater than `tell_epoch` (armed tells draw nothing, so the counter never exceeds the epoch count), WHEN restored, THEN restore returns `(ok false, ATTENTION_RESTORE_SCHEMA / _RANGE / _PHASE_INCOHERENT / _SECTION_UNKNOWN / _RNG_COUNTER, detail)`, nothing is clamped, and a restore round trip on a good block is idempotent. [AT-1, E-50]
- **AC-AT-97 (auto) Dirty keys.** GIVEN one run that changes `L` only and one that changes `phase`, WHEN the dirty set is read, THEN it is `{ATT/L}` and `{ATT/L, ATT/P}`; an element write into a sorted section array marks its key; the set never exceeds `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256. [AT-1]
- **AC-AT-71 (auto) Pause.** GIVEN a paused game, WHEN wall time passes, THEN sim `T` does not advance, Attention runs on `T` only and no catch-up beat or decay occurs on resume. [AT-28, E-52]
- **AC-AT-72 (auto; deferred: blocked on Modular Ship Assembly) Weld double count.** GIVEN a weld completing in a derelict welded to the home ship, WHEN the run executes, THEN zero `LOUD` is pushed at the weld completion (by Interaction or Assembly), and the next breach's `locus_body` spans both bodies; GIVEN a seam-cut reaching DONE, THEN exactly one `LOUD` (Interaction's, IA-33) is pushed and Assembly pushes none. [AT-4, E-44]
- **AC-AT-73 (auto) Hallucination boundary.** GIVEN a Sanity double that raises a phantom entity, a false "hull failing" state, a phantom emitter and phantom loot while `phase` is CALM, WHEN `attention.view`, `emitters()` and `TellAudit` are read, THEN the view reports CALM, `TellAudit` holds no event, nothing is added to `emitters()` or to the pushed noise, and no Attention symbol changed. [AT-17, E-53, E-54, E-57]
- **AC-AT-101 (source-lint) Determinism hygiene.** GIVEN the Attention and presenter sources, WHEN scanned, THEN no float type or literal, `randf`, `randi`, wall-clock call or `Dictionary` iteration appears in sim code; the only `rng_derive` call is the tell draw; beat jitter and presenter randomness call the integer hash. [AT-28, AT-20, F-12]
- **AC-AT-54a (spike) Engine arithmetic and storage.** GIVEN Godot 4.7.2 (the engine reference has no scripting or core file), WHEN a measured spike runs, THEN integer division and 64-bit arithmetic behave as the formulas assume (non-negative operands only), `RefCounted` block cost is acceptable and no behaviour relies on `Dictionary` iteration order (section-keyed arrays are stored sorted). [AT-1, AT-28]
- **AC-AT-54b (auto, performance; budget provisional: the technical-director confirms) Per-run cost.** GIVEN 96 emitters, 14 loaded sections and six seats pushing, WHEN `attention.step` runs 10,000 times on the reference machine, THEN p99 cost is at most 0.05 ms and no run exceeds `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4; the `attention.state` snapshot size stays within the bound Save/Load sets. Under `tests/performance/attention/`. [AT-1, AT-28]

### H. Cross-system discharge

- **AC-AT-74 (review) LSG:1063 discharged.** GIVEN this GDD and the Horror Entities GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN every call site of M17 RUPTURE, GOUGE and STRESS and every environmental M18 EXOGENOUS source is preceded by Attention's tell, named in AT-15, and none relies on the Graph's gate. The Attention side can be evaluated now; the Horror side is **deferred: blocked on Horror Entities**. [LSG:1063]
- **AC-AT-75 (review) AC-SL-121 discharged.** GIVEN this GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN `attention.view` carries `tell_active`, `tell_remaining` and `breach_phase` with the exact names and no field is `PERCEPTION_OUT`-derived. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-76 (review) EM AC-60 discharged.** GIVEN the registrations, WHEN reviewed, THEN Attention declares `after [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]`, Horror (when written; **deferred: blocked on Horror Entities**) declares `after` Attention and `emitter.place`, and a lit lamp counts once as light and not also in `lamps_lit_w`. [AT-1, AT-4, EM AC-60]
- **AC-AT-77 (review; deferred: blocked on Horror Entities) Horror must-repeats.** GIVEN the Horror Entities GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN it repeats the predicate `exogenous_permitted`, the crew-body scope, a per-run footprint budget of at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP - STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`, and the wiring of its single MVP behaviour to BREACH, or it surfaces a CONFLICT; the wiring is a **BLOCKING** dependency of Attention's breach teeth (gate ruling), and the GDD also provides a non-lethal presence or stalk outside BREACH so that CALM is never known-safe. [AT-11, AT-15, C22]
- **AC-AT-78 (review; deferred: blocked on Adaptive Audio & Tells) Adaptive Audio must-repeats.** GIVEN the Adaptive Audio & Tells GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN it repeats: the non-positional bed audible to every living player; reading `phase` not `breach_phase`; the dedupe key `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)`; no catch-up burst; the abort release cue; the alarm cadence and fallback (M1); mix priority (M3); the breach-entry loudness bound (M4); `phase == CALM` never an all-clear; no music change before beat 0; and a family disjoint from the Graph's, Horror's and Sanity's audio (M6). [AT-9, AT-12, AT-26, AUD-1 to AUD-10]
- **AC-AT-79 (review, at the confirming `/review-all-gdds` or consistency scan) Neighbour refreshes.** GIVEN the refresh list (C1 to C25), WHEN the stale-token list in Cross-References is run over every refresh target, THEN it returns zero hits in each (the count and the targets scanned are recorded; a bare zero is not a result), and a diff of the neighbours shows no value changed by this GDD. [Cross-References]
- **AC-AT-80 (playtest, non-blocking) The valley and fatigue.** GIVEN at least 8 recorded sessions of 25 to 35 minutes, WHEN analysed from the AT-32 counters, THEN: *abort share* (aborts / (aborts + breaches)) is 30 to 60 percent; experienced players abort at most 75 percent of tells (this is the acceptance target; the 85 percent of E-38 is the escalation tripwire at which the freeze is judged a ritual and `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` or `REFRACT_TICKS` is pulled); fewer than 20 percent of *sorties* (the interval between leaving the home ship and returning) show 3 or more consecutive aborts; *calm time* (ticks in CALM / total ticks) is at least 20 percent over a reckless trace; and both *Moment A* (the player stops every input after the level crosses `theta` and the tell aborts) and *Moment B* (the player keeps working through a tell and a breach commits) occur in at least 6 of 8 sessions (the Moment-ordering guard, the tripwire for commit-dominance, AT-11). Retained as a playtest report in `production/qa/evidence/`. [Player Fantasy, F-25]

### Gaps / untestable as written

1. **AC-AT-31** needs a named Graph read for the STRESS armed state; LSG line 526 describes a per-section state field, not a published query. Until the Graph owner names one it reads the block through a test-only accessor (OQ-AT-6).
2. **Deferred integration items** (AC-AT-46 notification name, 68, 72, 74 and 76 Horror halves, 77, 78, the audio half of AC-AT-47, AC-AT-109) stay open in the sprint-review blocker list until Web Map, Horror Entities, Modular Ship Assembly, Adaptive Audio & Tells and Sanity exist. Under the project evidence rule an Integration story needs an integration test or a documented playtest; a deferred one has neither yet.
3. **AC-AT-54b** has no budget until the technical-director sets it (0.05 ms is the primary's proposal, not a measurement).
4. **AC-AT-21's beat-count band (19 to 22)** is the qa-lead's proposal against the closed form 20.6; the tester confirms it against the integer model before the criterion is final.
5. **Player Fantasy's Moment A wording** no longer carries tick arithmetic (gate legibility ruling): it says only that an abort usually costs the partial, the price of restraint chosen on purpose. The CR-TIMELINE numerics (480 + 114 = 594 ticks, six inside `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`) live in F-15b and are pinned by AC-AT-105, so a retune shows as a diff.
6. **F-25's occupancy sentence** was corrected (22.6 percent is inside the 15 to 35 percent calm-time target and above AC-AT-80's 20 percent floor).
7. **Lint negative fixtures** are listed where the qa-lead asked for them; the data-lints without one named (AC-AT-52 and AC-AT-64 excepted) take their fixtures from the first out-of-range row of Tuning Knobs.
8. **Not testable here by design:** the dB audibility margin of the bed (M2, a listening test owned by Adaptive Audio), beam strobing in sync with the cadence (E-20: the Controller adapter's debounce owns its criterion), and the world-side photosensitivity ceiling (the accessibility-specialist confirms).

*Nothing decided in this document is reopened here. Rows are the routed asks, the flagged ADDITIONS awaiting a gate and the provisional neighbours. Owner: **user** = needs the user's decision, **agent** = a named specialist or neighbouring GDD owner, **GDD** = this document resolves it at a later pass. Target: playtest, next GDD, ADR, or a gate. The user-decision items come first; each carries the proposed default this GDD already follows.*

Derelict Authoring proposes that a body passes if it holds a fault that wakes when fed (A: `j_a_main` once its bus is served), or that a dead-bus derelict is exempt as the home ship is. A noise-only locus on an unserved A would then have no endogenous damage for STRESS to amplify (only Horror's BREACH behaviour).
